SilentlyContinue -TypeDefinition @\"\n     8→using System;\n     9→using System.Collections.Generic;\n    10→using System.Runtime.InteropServices;\n    11→using System.Text;\n    12→using System.Diagnostics;\n    13→\n    14→public class WindowLayoutAPI {\n    15→    public delegate bool EnumWindowsProc(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr lParam);\n    16→\n    17→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    18→    public static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumWindowsProc lpEnumFunc, IntPtr lParam);\n    19→\n    20→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", CharSet = CharSet.Auto)]\n    21→    public static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr hWnd, StringBuilder lpString, int nMaxCount);\n    22→\n    23→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    24→    public static extern bool IsWindowVisible(IntPtr hWnd);\n    25→\n    26→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", SetLastError = true)]\n    27→    public static extern bool SetWindowPos(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr hWndInsertAfter, int X, int Y, int cx, int cy, uint uFlags);\n    28→\n    29→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", SetLastError = true)]\n    30→    public static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr hWnd, out RECT lpRect);\n    31→\n    32→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", SetLastError = true)]\n    33→    public static extern int GetWindowLong(IntPtr hWnd, int nIndex);\n    34→\n    35→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", SetLastError = true)]\n    36→    public static extern int SetWindowLong(IntPtr hWnd, int nIndex, int dwNewLong);\n    37→\n    38→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", SetLastError = true)]\n    39→    public static extern bool SetLayeredWindowAttributes(IntPtr hWnd, uint crKey, byte bAlpha, uint dwFlags);\n    40→\n    41→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", SetLastError = true)]\n    42→    public static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr hWnd, out uint lpdwProcessId);\n    43→\n    44→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    45→    public static extern bool ShowWindow(IntPtr hWnd, int nCmdShow);\n    46→\n    47→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    48→    public static extern bool IsIconic(IntPtr hWnd);\n    49→\n    50→    public const int SW_RESTORE = 9;\n    51→\n    52→    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]\n    53→    public struct RECT {\n    54→        public int Left, Top, Right, Bottom;\n    55→    }\n    56→\n    57→    public const uint SWP_NOZORDER = 0x0004;\n    58→    public const uint SWP_NOACTIVATE = 0x0010;\n    59→    public const uint SWP_SHOWWINDOW = 0x0040;\n    60→    public const int GWL_EXSTYLE = -20;\n    61→    public const int WS_EX_LAYERED = 0x80000;\n    62→    public const uint LWA_ALPHA = 0x2;\n    63→}\n    64→\"@\n    65→\n    66→# Load System.Windows.Forms for screen detection\n    67→Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n    68→\n    69→# --- VERBOSE LOGGING SYSTEM ---\n    70→\n    71→# Global toggle for verbose layout logging (set to $true to enable)\n    72→$script:LayoutEngineVerbose = $false\n    73→$script:LayoutLogFile = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\Documents\\Matrix\\layout_debug.log\"\n    74→\n    75→<#\n    76→.SYNOPSIS\n    77→    Write a log message for layout engine debugging.\n    78→\n    79→.DESCRIPTION\n    80→    Writes timestamped messages to both console (when verbose) and optional log file.\n    81→    Controlled by $script:LayoutEngineVerbose global variable.\n    82→    Integrates with US-009 diagnostic logging pattern.\n    83→\n    84→.PARAMETER Message\n    85→    The message to log\n    86→\n    87→.PARAMETER Level\n    88→    Log level: INFO, WARN, ERROR, DEBUG (default: INFO)\n    89→\n    90→.PARAMETER Force\n    91→    If set, writes message even when verbose mode is disabled (for warnings/errors)\n    92→\n    93→.EXAMPLE\n    94→    Write-LayoutLog \"Starting layout calculation\" -Level \"INFO\"\n    95→    Write-LayoutLog \"Invalid handle detected\" -Level \"WARN\" -Force\n    96→#>\n    97→function Write-LayoutLog {\n    98→    param(\n    99→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n   100→        [string]$Message,\n   101→\n   102→        [ValidateSet('INFO', 'WARN', 'ERROR', 'DEBUG')]\n   103→        [string]$Level = 'INFO',\n   104→\n   105→        [switch]$Force\n   106→    )\n   107→\n   108→    # Skip if verbose mode is disabled (unless Force or it's a warning/error)\n   109→    if (-not $script:LayoutEngineVerbose -and -not $Force -and $Level -notin @('WARN', 'ERROR')) {\n   110→        return\n   111→    }\n   112→\n   113→    $timestamp = Get-Date -Format \"yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.fff\"\n   114→    $logEntry = \"[$timestamp] [$Level] $Message\"\n   115→\n   116→    # Console output with color coding\n   117→    $color = switch ($Level) {\n   118→        'INFO'  { 'Gray' }\n   119→        'WARN'  { 'Yellow' }\n   120→        'ERROR' { 'Red' }\n   121→        'DEBUG' { 'DarkGray' }\n   122→    }\n   123→\n   124→    if ($script:LayoutEngineVerbose -or $Force -or $Level -in @('WARN', 'ERROR')) {\n   125→        Write-Host $logEntry -ForegroundColor $color\n   126→    }\n   127→\n   128→    # File logging (only when verbose is enabled)\n   129→    if ($script:LayoutEngineVerbose) {\n   130→        try {\n   131→            # Ensure log directory exists\n   132→            $logDir = Split-Path $script:LayoutLogFile -Parent\n   133→            if (-not (Test-Path $logDir)) {\n   134→                New-Item -Path $logDir -ItemType Directory -Force | Out-Null\n   135→            }\n   136→            Add-Content -Path $script:LayoutLogFile -Value $logEntry -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n   137→        }\n   138→        catch {\n   139→            # Silently fail file logging - don't interrupt layout operations\n   140→        }\n   141→    }\n   142→}\n   143→\n   144→<#\n   145→.SYNOPSIS\n   146→    Enable verbose layout logging.\n   147→\n   148→.DESCRIPTION\n   149→    Sets the LayoutEngineVerbose flag and optionally clears the log file.\n   150→\n   151→.PARAMETER ClearLog\n   152→    If set, clears the existing log file\n   153→\n   154→.EXAMPLE\n   155→    Enable-LayoutVerboseLogging -ClearLog\n   156→#>\n   157→function Enable-LayoutVerboseLogging {\n   158→    param(\n   159→        [switch]$ClearLog\n   160→    )\n   161→\n   162→    $script:LayoutEngineVerbose = $true\n   163→    Write-LayoutLog \"Verbose logging ENABLED\" -Level \"INFO\" -Force\n   164→\n   165→    if ($ClearLog -and (Test-Path $script:LayoutLogFile)) {\n   166→        Remove-Item $script:LayoutLogFile -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n   167→        Write-LayoutLog \"Log file cleared\" -Level \"INFO\" -Force\n   168→    }\n   169→}\n   170→\n   171→<#\n   172→.SYNOPSIS\n   173→    Disable verbose layout logging.\n   174→\n   175→.EXAMPLE\n   176→    Disable-LayoutVerboseLogging\n   177→#>\n   178→function Disable-LayoutVerboseLogging {\n   179→    Write-LayoutLog \"Verbose logging DISABLED\" -Level \"INFO\" -Force\n   180→    $script:LayoutEngineVerbose = $false\n   181→}\n   182→\n   183→# --- SCREEN TOPOLOGY DETECTION ---\n   184→\n   185→<#\n   186→.SYNOPSIS\n   187→    Detect all monitors and return their working areas (excludes taskbar).\n   188→\n   189→.DESCRIPTION\n   190→    Returns an array of screen objects with index, position, size, and primary flag.\n   191→    Working area excludes the taskbar and other docked windows.\n   192→\n   193→.OUTPUTS\n   194→    Array of @{ Index, Left, Top, Width, Height, IsPrimary }\n   195→\n   196→.EXAMPLE\n   197→    $screens = Get-ScreenTopology\n   198→    # Returns: @(\n   199→    #   @{ Index=0; Left=0; Top=0; Width=1920; Height=1040; IsPrimary=$true },\n   200→    #   @{ Index=1; Left=1920; Top=0; Width=1920; Height=1080; IsPrimary=$false }\n   201→    # )\n   202→#>\n   203→function Get-ScreenTopology {\n   204→    try {\n   205→        $allScreens = [System.Windows.Forms.Screen]::AllScreens\n   206→        $topology = @()\n   207→\n   208→        for ($i = 0; $i -lt $allScreens.Count; $i++) {\n   209→            $screen = $allScreens[$i]\n   210→            $workingArea = $screen.WorkingArea\n   211→\n   212→            $topology += @{\n   213→                Index = $i\n   214→                Left = $workingArea.X\n   215→                Top = $workingArea.Y\n   216→                Width = $workingArea.Width\n   217→                Height = $workingArea.Height\n   218→                IsPrimary = $screen.Primary\n   219→            }\n   220→        }\n   221→\n   222→        # Sort screens: Primary first, then left-to-right by position\n   223→        # This ensures Windows' actual primary monitor is always index 0\n   224→        $sorted = $topology | Sort-Object -Property @(\n   225→            @{Expression = {-[int]$_.IsPrimary}; Ascending = $true},  # Primary first (IsPrimary=true sorts before false)\n   226→            @{Expression = {$_.Left}; Ascending = $true}               # Then left-to-right\n   227→        )\n   228→\n   229→        # Re-index after sorting so Index matches position in array\n   230→        for ($i = 0; $i -lt $sorted.Count; $i++) {\n   231→            $sorted[$i].Index = $i\n   232→        }\n   233→\n   234→        Write-LayoutLog \"Screen topology: $($sorted.Count) screens, primary at index 0\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n   235→        return $sorted\n   236→    }\n   237→    catch {\n   238→        Write-Warning \"Failed to detect screen topology: $_\"\n   239→        # Fallback to primary screen only\n   240→        $primary = [System.Windows.Forms.Screen]::PrimaryScreen.WorkingArea\n   241→        return @(\n   242→            @{\n   243→                Index = 0\n   244→                Left = $primary.X\n   245→                Top = $primary.Y\n   246→                Width = $primary.Width\n   247→                Height = $primary.Height\n   248→                IsPrimary = $true\n   249→            }\n   250→        )\n   251→    }\n   252→}\n   253→\n   254→# --- WINDOW DISTRIBUTION ALGORITHM ---\n   255→\n   256→<#\n   257→.SYNOPSIS\n   258→    Distribute N windows across M screens with a maximum per screen.\n   259→\n   260→.DESCRIPTION\n   261→    Evenly distributes windows across available screens, respecting MaxPerScreen limit.\n   262→    Algorithm: floor division with remainder distributed to first screens.\n   263→\n   264→.PARAMETER WindowCount\n   265→    Total number of windows to distribute\n   266→\n   267→.PARAMETER ScreenCount\n   268→    Number of available screens\n   269→\n   270→.PARAMETER MaxPerScreen\n   271→    Maximum windows allowed per screen (default: 4)\n   272→\n   273→.OUTPUTS\n   274→    Array of integers representing window count per screen\n   275→\n   276→.EXAMPLE\n   277→    Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount 4 -ScreenCount 1 -MaxPerScreen 4\n   278→    # Returns: @(4)\n   279→\n   280→.EXAMPLE\n   281→    Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount 5 -ScreenCount 1 -MaxPerScreen 4\n   282→    # Returns: @(4) - overflow not handled in distribution, handled by layout strategy\n   283→\n   284→.EXAMPLE\n   285→    Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount 8 -ScreenCount 2 -MaxPerScreen 4\n   286→    # Returns: @(4, 4)\n   287→\n   288→.EXAMPLE\n   289→    Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount 3 -ScreenCount 2 -MaxPerScreen 4\n   290→    # Returns: @(2, 1)\n   291→#>\n   292→function Get-WindowDistribution {\n   293→    param(\n   294→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n   295→        [int]$WindowCount,\n   296→\n   297→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n   298→        [int]$ScreenCount,\n   299→\n   300→        [int]$MaxPerScreen = 4\n   301→    )\n   302→\n   303→    if ($WindowCount -le 0) {\n   304→        return @(0) * $ScreenCount\n   305→    }\n   306→\n   307→    if ($ScreenCount -le 0) {\n   308→        Write-Warning \"Invalid ScreenCount: $ScreenCount\"\n   309→        return @()\n   310→    }\n   311→\n   312→    # Initialize distribution array\n   313→    $distribution = @(0) * $ScreenCount\n   314→\n   315→    # Special case: single screen - distribute ALL windows to it (allow overflow for multi-row)\n   316→    if ($ScreenCount -eq 1) {\n   317→        $distribution[0] = $WindowCount\n   318→        return $distribution\n   319→    }\n   320→\n   321→    # Multi-screen distribution: balance windows across screens\n   322→    # Base windows per screen (floor division)\n   323→    $windowsPerScreen = [Math]::Floor($WindowCount / $ScreenCount)\n   324→\n   325→    # Cap at MaxPerScreen for balanced distribution\n   326→    $windowsPerScreen = [Math]::Min($windowsPerScreen, $MaxPerScreen)\n   327→\n   328→    # Remainder to distribute\n   329→    $remainder = $WindowCount % $ScreenCount\n   330→\n   331→    # Distribute base count to all screens\n   332→    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $ScreenCount; $i++) {\n   333→        $distribution[$i] = $windowsPerScreen\n   334→    }\n   335→\n   336→    # Distribute remainder to first screens (round-robin)\n   337→    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $remainder; $i++) {\n   338→        $screenIndex = $i % $ScreenCount\n   339→        if ($distribution[$screenIndex] -lt $MaxPerScreen) {\n   340→            $distribution[$screenIndex]++\n   341→        }\n   342→        else {\n   343→            # If first screen is full, find next available screen\n   344→            for ($j = 0; $j -lt $ScreenCount; $j++) {\n   345→                if ($distribution[$j] -lt $MaxPerScreen) {\n   346→                    $distribution[$j]++\n   347→                    break\n   348→                }\n   349→            }\n   350→        }\n   351→    }\n   352→\n   353→    return $distribution\n   354→}\n   355→\n   356→<#\n   357→.SYNOPSIS\n   358→    Distribute windows with user-controlled primary screen allocation.\n   359→\n   360→.DESCRIPTION\n   361→    Distributes windows across screens, respecting the WindowsOnPrimary setting.\n   362→    Primary screen (index 0) gets the specified number of windows first,\n   363→    then overflow goes to secondary screens.\n   364→\n   365→    Examples:\n   366→    - WindowsOnPrimary=3, Total=5: Primary gets 3, Secondary gets 2\n   367→    - WindowsOnPrimary=null (Auto): All windows on primary (single-screen behavior)\n   368→    - WindowsOnPrimary=0: All windows on secondary screens\n   369→\n   370→.PARAMETER WindowCount\n   371→    Total number of windows to distribute\n   372→\n   373→.PARAMETER ScreenCount\n   374→    Number of available screens\n   375→\n   376→.PARAMETER WindowsOnPrimary\n   377→    Number of windows to place on primary screen (index 0).\n   378→    - null or -1: Auto mode - all windows on primary (backward compatible)\n   379→    - 0: All windows on secondary screens\n   380→    - N: Exactly N windows on primary, rest overflow to secondary\n   381→\n   382→.PARAMETER MaxPerScreen\n   383→    Maximum windows allowed per screen (default: 4)\n   384→\n   385→.OUTPUTS\n   386→    Array of integers representing window count per screen\n   387→\n   388→.EXAMPLE\n   389→    Get-WindowDistributionWithPrimary -WindowCount 5 -ScreenCount 2 -WindowsOnPrimary 3 -MaxPerScreen 4\n   390→    # Returns: @(3, 2) - 3 on primary, 2 on secondary\n   391→\n   392→.EXAMPLE\n   393→    Get-WindowDistributionWithPrimary -WindowCount 4 -ScreenCount 2 -WindowsOnPrimary $null -MaxPerScreen 4\n   394→    # Returns: @(4, 0) - Auto mode, all on primary\n   395→#>\n   396→function Get-WindowDistributionWithPrimary {\n   397→    param(\n   398→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n   399→        [int]$WindowCount,\n   400→\n   401→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n   402→        [int]$ScreenCount,\n   403→\n   404→        [object]$WindowsOnPrimary = $null,  # Use [object] to allow $null, or an integer\n   405→\n   406→        [int]$MaxPerScreen = 4\n   407→    )\n   408→\n   409→    $typeStr = if ($null -eq $WindowsOnPrimary) { \"null\" } else { $WindowsOnPrimary.GetType().Name }\n   410→    Write-LayoutLog \"Get-WindowDistributionWithPrimary: WindowCount=$WindowCount, ScreenCount=$ScreenCount, WindowsOnPrimary=$WindowsOnPrimary (type=$typeStr)\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n   411→\n   412→    if ($WindowCount -le 0) {\n   413→        return @(0) * $ScreenCount\n   414→    }\n   415→\n   416→    if ($ScreenCount -le 0) {\n   417→        Write-Warning \"Invalid ScreenCount: $ScreenCount\"\n   418→        return @()\n   419→    }\n   420→\n   421→    # Initialize distribution array\n   422→    $distribution = @(0) * $ScreenCount\n   423→\n   424→    # Single screen - put everything there\n   425→    if ($ScreenCount -eq 1) {\n   426→        $distribution[0] = $WindowCount\n   427→        Write-LayoutLog \"Single screen: all $WindowCount windows on screen 0\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n   428→        return $distribution\n   429→    }\n   430→\n   431→    # Multi-screen distribution\n   432→    # Auto mode (null): Balanced distribution with max 4 per screen\n   433→    # User-specified: Put exactly N on primary, rest on secondary\n   434→\n   435→    if ($null -eq $WindowsOnPrimary) {\n   436→        # AUTO MODE: Balanced distribution across screens\n   437→        # Divide evenly, primary gets remainder (e.g., 5 windows / 2 screens = 3 + 2)\n   438→        $base = [Math]::Floor($WindowCount / $ScreenCount)\n   439→        $remainder = $WindowCount % $ScreenCount\n   440→\n   441→        for ($i = 0; $i -lt $ScreenCount; $i++) {\n   442→            # Each screen gets base count, first screens get +1 for remainder\n   443→            $count = $base\n   444→            if ($i -lt $remainder) {\n   445→                $count++\n   446→            }\n   447→            # Cap at MaxPerScreen\n   448→            $distribution[$i] = [Math]::Min($count, $MaxPerScreen)\n   449→        }\n   450→\n   451→        Write-LayoutLog \"Auto mode: balanced distribution $($distribution -join ', ') (base=$base, remainder=$remainder)\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n   452→    }\n   453→    else {\n   454→        # User-specified primary allocation\n   455→        $primaryCount = [Math]::Max(0, [Math]::Min([int]$WindowsOnPrimary, $WindowCount))\n   456→        $distribution[0] = $primaryCount\n   457→        $remaining = $WindowCount - $primaryCount\n   458→\n   459→        Write-LayoutLog \"User mode: primary gets $primaryCount (user specified $WindowsOnPrimary), $remaining remaining\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n   460→\n   461→        # Distribute remaining windows to secondary screens (index 1+)\n   462→        if ($remaining -gt 0 -and $ScreenCount -gt 1) {\n   463→            $secondaryScreens = $ScreenCount - 1\n   464→            $perSecondary = [Math]::Ceiling($remaining / $secondaryScreens)\n   465→            $perSecondary = [Math]::Min($perSecondary, $MaxPerScreen)\n   466→\n   467→            for ($i = 1; $i -lt $ScreenCount; $i++) {\n   468→                if ($remaining -le 0) { break }\n   469→                $windowsForThisScreen = [Math]::Min($remaining, $perSecondary)\n   470→                $distribution[$i] = $windowsForThisScreen\n   471→                $remaining -= $windowsForThisScreen\n   472→            }\n   473→        }\n   474→    }\n   475→\n   476→    Write-LayoutLog \"Final distribution: $($distribution -join ', ')\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n   477→    return $distribution\n   478→}\n   479→\n   480→# --- PILLARS LAYOUT STRATEGY ---\n   481→\n   482→<#\n   483→.SYNOPSIS\n   484→    Calculate Pillars layout: vertical columns arranged side-by-side.\n   485→\n   486→.DESCRIPTION\n   487→    Arranges windows as vertical pillars (columns) with configurable gaps.\n   488→    Supports multi-row overflow and multi-monitor distribution.\n   489→\n   490→    Layout logic:\n   491→    - If windowsOnScreen <= MaxPillarsPerScreen: single row of N columns\n   492→    - If windowsOnScreen > MaxPillarsPerScreen: grid with MaxPillarsPerScreen columns, multiple rows\n   493→    - Gap spacing: (N+1) gaps for N columns, (M+1) gaps for M rows\n   494→\n   495→.PARAMETER WindowCount\n   496→    Total number of windows to layout\n   497→\n   498→.PARAMETER Screens\n   499→    Array of screen objects from Get-ScreenTopology\n   500→\n   501→.PARAMETER MaxPillarsPerScreen\n   502→    Maximum pillars (columns) per screen in a single row (default: 4)\n   503→\n   504→.PARAMETER GapSize\n   505→    Pixel gap between windows and screen edges (default: 60)\n   506→\n   507→.OUTPUTS\n   508→    Array of @{ X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex, WindowIndex }\n   509→\n   510→.EXAMPLE\n   511→    Get-PillarsLayout -WindowCount 4 -Screens $screens -MaxPillarsPerScreen 4 -GapSize 60\n   512→    # Returns 4 rectangles in a single row, evenly distributed with 60px gaps\n   513→#>\n   514→function Get-PillarsLayout {\n   515→    param(\n   516→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n   517→        [int]$WindowCount,\n   518→\n   519→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n   520→        [AllowEmptyCollection()]\n   521→        [array]$Screens,\n   522→\n   523→        [int]$MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4,\n   524→\n   525→        [int]$GapSize = 60,\n   526→\n   527→        [object]$WindowsOnPrimary = $null  # Use [object] to allow $null\n   528→    )\n   529→\n   530→    # Enforce minimum gap to ensure visible separation between windows\n   531→    $GapSize = [Math]::Max($GapSize, 30)\n   532→\n   533→    Write-LayoutLog \"Get-PillarsLayout called: WindowCount=$WindowCount, ScreenCount=$($Screens.Count), MaxPillars=$MaxPillarsPerScreen, GapSize=$GapSize, WindowsOnPrimary=$WindowsOnPrimary\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n   534→\n   535→    if ($WindowCount -le 0) {\n   536→        Write-LayoutLog \"Get-PillarsLayout: Zero or negative window count, returning empty\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n   537→        return @()\n   538→    }\n   539→\n   540→    if (-not $Screens -or $Screens.Count -eq 0) {\n   541→        Write-LayoutLog \"No screens available for Pillars layout\" -Level \"WARN\"\n   542→        return @()\n   543→    }\n   544→\n   545→    # Step 1: Distribute windows across screens (respects WindowsOnPrimary setting)\n   546→    $distribution = Get-WindowDistributionWithPrimary -WindowCount $WindowCount `\n   547→                                                      -ScreenCount $Screens.Count `\n   548→                                                      -WindowsOnPrimary $WindowsOnPrimary `\n   549→                                                      -MaxPerScreen $MaxPillarsPerScreen\n   550→\n   551→    $rectangles = @()\n   552→    $windowIndex = 0\n   553→\n   554→    # Step 2: For each screen, layout its assigned windows\n   555→    for ($screenIdx = 0; $screenIdx -lt $Screens.Count; $screenIdx++) {\n   556→        $screen = $Screens[$screenIdx]\n   557→        $windowsOnScreen = $distribution[$screenIdx]\n   558→\n   559→        if ($windowsOnScreen -eq 0) {\n   560→            continue\n   561→        }\n   562→\n   563→        # Step 3: Calculate pillar dimensions based on ACTUAL window count\n   564→        # Pillars fill the available space while maintaining tall aspect ratio\n   565→        $columns = [Math]::Min($windowsOnScreen, $MaxPillarsPerScreen)\n   566→        $rows = 1\n   567→\n   568→        # If more windows than fit in one row, use multi-row\n   569→        if ($windowsOnScreen -gt $MaxPillarsPerScreen) {\n   570→            $columns = $MaxPillarsPerScreen\n   571→            $rows = [Math]::Ceiling($windowsOnScreen / $columns)\n   572→        }\n   573→\n   574→        # Step 4: Calculate cell dimensions to FILL the screen\n   575→        # Formula: (N+1) gaps for N columns/rows\n   576→        $totalHGaps = ($columns + 1) * $GapSize\n   577→        $totalVGaps = ($rows + 1) * $GapSize\n   578→        $cellWidth = [int](($screen.Width - $totalHGaps) / $columns)\n   579→        $cellHeight = [int](($screen.Height - $totalVGaps) / $rows)\n   580→\n   581→        # Step 5: Place each window in grid\n   582→        for ($i = 0; $i -lt $windowsOnScreen; $i++) {\n   583→            # Calculate grid position (column-major order)\n   584→            $col = $i % $columns\n   585→            $row = [Math]::Floor($i / $columns)\n   586→\n   587→            # Calculate pixel position\n   588→            # Formula: edge gap + (cell_index * (cell_size + gap))\n   589→            $x = $screen.Left + $GapSize + ($col * ($cellWidth + $GapSize))\n   590→            $y = $screen.Top + $GapSize + ($row * ($cellHeight + $GapSize))\n   591→\n   592→            $rectangles += @{\n   593→                X = $x\n   594→                Y = $y\n   595→                Width = $cellWidth\n   596→                Height = $cellHeight\n   597→                ScreenIndex = $screenIdx\n   598→                WindowIndex = $windowIndex++\n   599→            }\n   600→        }\n   601→    }\n   602→\n   603→    # Always return as array to prevent single-item unwrapping\n   604→    # Use write-output with -NoEnumerate to preserve array structure\n   605→    Write-Output $rectangles -NoEnumerate\n   606→}\n   607→\n   608→# --- QUADS LAYOUT STRATEGY ---\n   609→\n   610→<#\n   611→.SYNOPSIS\n   612→    Calculate Quads layout: 2x2 grid with plus-shaped gap in center.\n   613→\n   614→.DESCRIPTION\n   615→    Arranges windows in a 2x2 grid pattern with a distinctive plus-shaped (+) gap\n   616→    in the center where horizontal and vertical gaps intersect.\n   617→\n   618→    Layout pattern (single screen, 4 windows):\n   619→    ┌─────┐ GAP ┌─────┐\n   620→    │  1  │     │  2  │\n   621→    └─────┘     └─────┘\n   622→       GAP   +   GAP\n   623→    ┌─────┐     ┌─────┘\n   624→    │  3  │     │  4  │\n   625→    └─────┘     └─────┘\n   626→\n   627→    Window placement order: Top-Left, Top-Right, Bottom-Left, Bottom-Right\n   628→\n   629→    For fewer than 4 windows:\n   630→    - 1 window: Top-Left only\n   631→    - 2 windows: Top row (TL, TR)\n   632→    - 3 windows: Top row + Bottom-Left\n   633→\n   634→    For more than 4 windows:\n   635→    - Overflow to additional screens (if available)\n   636→    - Each screen gets up to 4 windows in quad pattern\n   637→\n   638→.PARAMETER WindowCount\n   639→    Total number of windows to layout\n   640→\n   641→.PARAMETER Screens\n   642→    Array of screen objects from Get-ScreenTopology\n   643→\n   644→.PARAMETER GapSize\n   645→    Pixel gap between windows and screen edges (default: 60)\n   646→    This creates the plus-shaped gap in the center\n   647→\n   648→.OUTPUTS\n   649→    Array of @{ X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex, WindowIndex }\n   650→\n   651→.EXAMPLE\n   652→    Get-QuadsLayout -WindowCount 4 -Screens $screens -GapSize 60\n   653→    # Returns 4 rectangles in 2x2 grid with 60px plus-gap\n   654→\n   655→.EXAMPLE\n   656→    Get-QuadsLayout -WindowCount 2 -Screens $screens -GapSize 60\n   657→    # Returns 2 rectangles in top row only\n   658→\n   659→.NOTES\n   660→    Plus-gap calculation:\n   661→    - Each quadrant: (screen_dimension - 3*gap) / 2\n   662→    - Three gaps: left edge, center, right edge (or top, center, bottom)\n   663→    - Center gap creates the distinctive + pattern\n   664→#>\n   665→function Get-QuadsLayout {\n   666→    param(\n   667→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n   668→        [int]$WindowCount,\n   669→\n   670→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n   671→        [AllowEmptyCollection()]\n   672→        [array]$Screens,\n   673→\n   674→        [int]$GapSize = 60,\n   675→\n   676→        [object]$WindowsOnPrimary = $null  # Use [object] to allow $null\n   677→    )\n   678→\n   679→    # Enforce minimum gap to ensure visible separation between windows\n   680→    $GapSize = [Math]::Max($GapSize, 30)\n   681→\n   682→    Write-LayoutLog \"Get-QuadsLayout called: WindowCount=$WindowCount, ScreenCount=$($Screens.Count), GapSize=$GapSize, WindowsOnPrimary=$WindowsOnPrimary\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n   683→\n   684→    if ($WindowCount -le 0) {\n   685→        Write-LayoutLog \"Get-QuadsLayout: Zero or negative window count, returning empty\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n   686→        return @()\n   687→    }\n   688→\n   689→    if (-not $Screens -or $Screens.Count -eq 0) {\n   690→        Write-LayoutLog \"No screens available for Quads layout\" -Level \"WARN\"\n   691→        return @()\n   692→    }\n   693→\n   694→    $rectangles = @()\n   695→    $windowIndex = 0\n   696→    $windowsPerQuad = 4\n   697→\n   698→    # Get distribution using WindowsOnPrimary setting\n   699→    $distribution = Get-WindowDistributionWithPrimary -WindowCount $WindowCount `\n   700→                                                      -ScreenCount $Screens.Count `\n   701→                                                      -WindowsOnPrimary $WindowsOnPrimary `\n   702→                                                      -MaxPerScreen $windowsPerQuad\n   703→\n   704→    # Calculate total capacity (4 windows per screen in standard quad layout)\n   705→    $totalCapacity = $Screens.Count * $windowsPerQuad\n   706→\n   707→    # Handle overflow: if more windows than quad capacity, use extended grid layout\n   708→    if ($WindowCount -gt $totalCapacity) {\n   709→        Write-LayoutLog \"Quads overflow: $WindowCount windows > $totalCapacity capacity, using extended grid layout\" -Level \"INFO\"\n   710→\n   711→        # Fall back to a grid layout that can handle all windows\n   712→        for ($screenIdx = 0; $screenIdx -lt $Screens.Count; $screenIdx++) {\n   713→            $windowsOnThisScreen = $distribution[$screenIdx]\n   714→            if ($windowsOnThisScreen -le 0) {\n   715→                continue\n   716→            }\n   717→\n   718→            $screen = $Screens[$screenIdx]\n   719→\n   720→            # Calculate grid dimensions to fit all windows\n   721→            # Try to maintain roughly square cells\n   722→            $cols = [Math]::Ceiling([Math]::Sqrt($windowsOnThisScreen * ($screen.Width / $screen.Height)))\n   723→            $cols = [Math]::Max($cols, 2)  # At least 2 columns for quad-like appearance\n   724→            $rows = [Math]::Ceiling($windowsOnThisScreen / $cols)\n   725→\n   726→            Write-LayoutLog \"Screen ${screenIdx}: $windowsOnThisScreen windows in ${cols}x${rows} grid\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n   727→\n   728→            # Calculate cell dimensions with gaps\n   729→            $totalHGaps = ($cols + 1) * $GapSize\n   730→            $totalVGaps = ($rows + 1) * $GapSize\n   731→            $cellWidth = [int](($screen.Width - $totalHGaps) / $cols)\n   732→            $cellHeight = [int](($screen.Height - $totalVGaps) / $rows)\n   733→\n   734→            # Place windows in grid\n   735→            for ($i = 0; $i -lt $windowsOnThisScreen; $i++) {\n   736→                $col = $i % $cols\n   737→                $row = [Math]::Floor($i / $cols)\n   738→\n   739→                $x = $screen.Left + $GapSize + ($col * ($cellWidth + $GapSize))\n   740→                $y = $screen.Top + $GapSize + ($row * ($cellHeight + $GapSize))\n   741→\n   742→                $rectangles += @{\n   743→                    X = $x\n   744→                    Y = $y\n   745→                    Width = $cellWidth\n   746→                    Height = $cellHeight\n   747→                    ScreenIndex = $screenIdx\n   748→                    WindowIndex = $windowIndex++\n   749→                }\n   750→            }\n   751→        }\n   752→\n   753→        # Always return as array to prevent single-item unwrapping\n   754→        Write-Output $rectangles -NoEnumerate\n   755→        return\n   756→    }\n   757→\n   758→    # Standard quad layout (4 or fewer windows per screen)\n   759→    # Use pre-computed distribution from Get-WindowDistributionWithPrimary\n   760→    for ($screenIdx = 0; $screenIdx -lt $Screens.Count; $screenIdx++) {\n   761→        $windowsOnThisScreen = $distribution[$screenIdx]\n   762→        if ($windowsOnThisScreen -le 0) {\n   763→            continue\n   764→        }\n   765→\n   766→        $screen = $Screens[$screenIdx]\n   767→\n   768→        Write-LayoutLog \"Screen ${screenIdx}: $windowsOnThisScreen windows in quad positions\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n   769→\n   770→        # Plus-gap calculation:\n   771→        # Each dimension has 3 gaps: edge + center + edge\n   772→        # Two quadrants share the remaining space equally\n   773→        # Formula: (total - 3*gap) / 2\n   774→        $halfWidth = [int](($screen.Width - (3 * $GapSize)) / 2)\n   775→        $halfHeight = [int](($screen.Height - (3 * $GapSize)) / 2)\n   776→\n   777→        # Define quad positions: TL, TR, BL, BR\n   778→        $quadPositions = @(\n   779→            @{ X = $screen.Left + $GapSize; Y = $screen.Top + $GapSize; Label = \"TL\" },\n   780→            @{ X = $screen.Left + (2 * $GapSize) + $halfWidth; Y = $screen.Top + $GapSize; Label = \"TR\" },\n   781→            @{ X = $screen.Left + $GapSize; Y = $screen.Top + (2 * $GapSize) + $halfHeight; Label = \"BL\" },\n   782→            @{ X = $screen.Left + (2 * $GapSize) + $halfWidth; Y = $screen.Top + (2 * $GapSize) + $halfHeight; Label = \"BR\" }\n   783→        )\n   784→\n   785→        # Place windows in order: TL, TR, BL, BR\n   786→        for ($posIdx = 0; $posIdx -lt $windowsOnThisScreen; $posIdx++) {\n   787→            $rectangles += @{\n   788→                X = $quadPositions[$posIdx].X\n   789→                Y = $quadPositions[$posIdx].Y\n   790→                Width = $halfWidth\n   791→                Height = $halfHeight\n   792→                ScreenIndex = $screenIdx\n   793→                WindowIndex = $windowIndex++\n   794→            }\n   795→            Write-LayoutLog \"  Placed window $($windowIndex-1) at $($quadPositions[$posIdx].Label): ($($quadPositions[$posIdx].X), $($quadPositions[$posIdx].Y))\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n   796→        }\n   797→    }\n   798→\n   799→    Write-LayoutLog \"Get-QuadsLayout complete: $($rectangles.Count) rectangles calculated\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n   800→    # Always return as array to prevent single-item unwrapping\n   801→    Write-Output $rectangles -NoEnumerate\n   802→}\n   803→\n   804→# --- CONFIGURATION MANAGEMENT ---\n   805→\n   806→<#\n   807→.SYNOPSIS\n   808→    Read layout configuration from matrix_state.json.\n   809→\n   810→.DESCRIPTION\n   811→    Loads layout preferences from persistent state file. Returns defaults\n   812→    if config is missing or invalid. Implements US-002 error handling pattern.\n   813→\n   814→.OUTPUTS\n   815→    Hashtable with layout configuration:\n   816→    @{ Mode = 'Pillars'; MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4; GapSize = 60; PreferredScreen = 0 }\n   817→\n   818→.EXAMPLE\n   819→    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n   820→    # Returns: @{ Mode = 'Pillars'; MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4; GapSize = 60; PreferredScreen = 0 }\n   821→\n   822→.NOTES\n   823→    Default configuration:\n   824→    - Mode: Pillars (vertical columns layout)\n   825→    - MaxPillarsPerScreen: 4 (max columns per screen)\n   826→    - GapSize: 60 (pixels between windows and edges)\n   827→    - PreferredScreen: 0 (primary screen)\n   828→#>\n   829→function Get-MatrixLayoutConfig {\n   830→    $stateFilePath = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\Documents\\Matrix\\matrix_state.json\"\n   831→\n   832→    # Default configuration\n   833→    $defaultConfig = @{\n   834→        Mode = 'Pillars'\n   835→        MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4\n   836→        GapSize = 60\n   837→        PreferredScreen = 0\n   838→        WindowsOnPrimary = $null  # null = Auto (all windows on primary for single-screen behavior)\n   839→    }\n   840→\n   841→    # Try to load existing state (US-002 pattern: JSON error handling)\n   842→    try {\n   843→        if (Test-Path $stateFilePath) {\n   844→            $stateJson = Get-Content -Path $stateFilePath -Raw -ErrorAction Stop\n   845→            $state = $stateJson | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop\n   846→\n   847→            # Extract layout config if present\n   848→            if ($state.layout) {\n   849→                $config = @{\n   850→                    Mode = if ($state.layout.mode) { $state.layout.mode } else { $defaultConfig.Mode }\n   851→                    MaxPillarsPerScreen = if ($state.layout.maxPillarsPerScreen) { $state.layout.maxPillarsPerScreen } else { $defaultConfig.MaxPillarsPerScreen }\n   852→                    GapSize = if ($state.layout.gapSize) { $state.layout.gapSize } else { $defaultConfig.GapSize }\n   853→                    PreferredScreen = if ($state.layout.preferredScreen -ne $null) { $state.layout.preferredScreen } else { $defaultConfig.PreferredScreen }\n   854→                    WindowsOnPrimary = if ($null -ne $state.layout.windowsOnPrimary) { $state.layout.windowsOnPrimary } else { $defaultConfig.WindowsOnPrimary }\n   855→                }\n   856→                return $config\n   857→            }\n   858→        }\n   859→\n   860→        # No config found, return defaults\n   861→        return $defaultConfig\n   862→    }\n   863→    catch {\n   864→        Write-Warning \"Failed to load layout config from matrix_state.json: $_\"\n   865→        Write-Warning \"Using default configuration\"\n   866→        return $defaultConfig\n   867→    }\n   868→}\n   869→\n   870→<#\n   871→.SYNOPSIS\n   872→    Write layout configuration to matrix_state.json.\n   873→\n   874→.DESCRIPTION\n   875→    Persists layout preferences to state file. Uses atomic write pattern (US-001)\n   876→    to prevent corruption. Merges with existing state to preserve other data.\n   877→\n   878→.PARAMETER Config\n   879→    Hashtable with layout configuration (Mode, MaxPillarsPerScreen, GapSize, PreferredScreen)\n   880→\n   881→.EXAMPLE\n   882→    Set-MatrixLayoutConfig -Config @{ Mode = 'Quads'; GapSize = 80 }\n   883→\n   884→.NOTES\n   885→    Implements US-001 pattern: atomic write with Move-Item -Force\n   886→    Implements US-002 pattern: try-catch on JSON operations\n   887→    Preserves existing state data (lastSlots, windows array, etc.)\n   888→#>\n   889→function Set-MatrixLayoutConfig {\n   890→    param(\n   891→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n   892→        [hashtable]$Config\n   893→    )\n   894→\n   895→    $matrixDir = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\Documents\\Matrix\"\n   896→    $stateFilePath = \"$matrixDir\\matrix_state.json\"\n   897→\n   898→    try {\n   899→        # Ensure directory exists\n   900→        if (-not (Test-Path $matrixDir)) {\n   901→            New-Item -Path $matrixDir -ItemType Directory -Force | Out-Null\n   902→        }\n   903→\n   904→        # Load existing state or create new (US-002 pattern)\n   905→        $state = @{}\n   906→        if (Test-Path $stateFilePath) {\n   907→            try {\n   908→                $stateJson = Get-Content -Path $stateFilePath -Raw -ErrorAction Stop\n   909→                $stateObj = $stateJson | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop\n   910→\n   911→                # Convert PSCustomObject to hashtable (preserve existing data)\n   912→                $state = @{}\n   913→                $stateObj.PSObject.Properties | ForEach-Object {\n   914→                    $state[$_.Name] = $_.Value\n   915→                }\n   916→            }\n   917→            catch {\n   918→                Write-Warning \"Failed to load existing state, creating new: $_\"\n   919→                $state = @{}\n   920→            }\n   921→        }\n   922→\n   923→        # Update layout section with provided config\n   924→        $state.layout = @{\n   925→            mode = if ($Config.Mode) { $Config.Mode } else { 'Pillars' }\n   926→            maxPillarsPerScreen = if ($Config.MaxPillarsPerScreen) { $Config.MaxPillarsPerScreen } else { 4 }\n   927→            gapSize = if ($Config.GapSize) { $Config.GapSize } else { 60 }\n   928→            preferredScreen = if ($Config.PreferredScreen -ne $null) { $Config.PreferredScreen } else { 0 }\n   929→            windowsOnPrimary = $Config.WindowsOnPrimary  # null = Auto (all on primary)\n   930→        }\n   931→\n   932→        # Convert to JSON\n   933→        $stateJson = $state | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10 -ErrorAction Stop\n   934→\n   935→        # Atomic write pattern (US-001): write to temp file, then move\n   936→        $tempFile = [System.IO.Path]::GetTempFileName()\n   937→        $stateJson | Out-File -FilePath $tempFile -Encoding UTF8 -ErrorAction Stop\n   938→        Move-Item -Path $tempFile -Destination $stateFilePath -Force -ErrorAction Stop\n   939→\n   940→        Write-Verbose \"Layout configuration saved successfully\"\n   941→    }\n   942→    catch {\n   943→        Write-Warning \"Failed to save layout config to matrix_state.json: $_\"\n   944→        # Clean up temp file if it exists\n   945→        if ($tempFile -and (Test-Path $tempFile)) {\n   946→            Remove-Item -Path $tempFile -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n   947→        }\n   948→        throw\n   949→    }\n   950→}\n   951→\n   952→# --- MAIN ENTRY POINT ---\n   953→\n   954→<#\n   955→.SYNOPSIS\n   956→    Calculate and apply window layout to Matrix Terminal windows.\n   957→\n   958→.DESCRIPTION\n   959→    Main entry point for the Window Layout Engine. Takes a collection of window handles\n   960→    and arranges them using either Pillars or Quads layout strategy.\n   961→\n   962→    Pillars mode: Windows arranged as vertical columns side-by-side\n   963→    Quads mode: Windows arranged in 2x2 grid with plus-shaped gap\n   964→    Auto mode: Pillars for 1-4 windows, Quads for 5+ windows\n   965→\n   966→.PARAMETER WindowHandles\n   967→    Hashtable where keys are shader names (e.g., \"Matrix-1\") and values are\n   968→    hashtables with Handle and optionally ProcessId:\n   969→    @{ \"Matrix-1\" = @{ Handle = 12345; ProcessId = 789 } }\n   970→\n   971→.PARAMETER Mode\n   972→    Layout strategy to use: 'Pillars', 'Quads', or 'Auto' (default)\n   973→    Auto selects Pillars for 1-4 windows, Quads for 5+ windows\n   974→\n   975→.PARAMETER DryRun\n   976→    If specified, calculates and returns layout without moving windows\n   977→\n   978→.OUTPUTS\n   979→    When DryRun: Array of @{ Handle, X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex }\n   980→    Otherwise: $null (windows are moved as side effect)\n   981→\n   982→.EXAMPLE\n   983→    Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowHandles $global:matrixWindowHandles -Mode 'Pillars'\n   984→    # Arranges all tracked windows in pillars layout\n   985→\n   986→.EXAMPLE\n   987→    $layout = Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowHandles $handles -DryRun\n   988→    # Returns calculated layout without moving windows\n   989→\n   990→.NOTES\n   991→    This function is the primary integration point for matrix_control.ps1,\n   992→    matrix_setup.ps1, and bluepill.ps1.\n   993→\n   994→    Uses configuration from Get-MatrixLayoutConfig when Mode is not specified.\n   995→    Handles edge cases: zero windows, invalid handles, minimized windows.\n   996→#>\n   997→function Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout {\n   998→    param(\n   999→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n  1000→        [hashtable]$WindowHandles,\n  1001→\n  1002→        [ValidateSet('Pillars', 'Quads', 'Auto')]\n  1003→        [string]$Mode = 'Auto',\n  1004→\n  1005→        [switch]$DryRun\n  1006→    )\n  1007→\n  1008→    Write-LayoutLog \"Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout called: Mode=$Mode, DryRun=$DryRun, InputHandles=$($WindowHandles.Count)\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n  1009→\n  1010→    # Get valid window handles (filter out any invalid ones)\n  1011→    $validWindows = @()\n  1012→    $invalidCount = 0\n  1013→    foreach ($name in $WindowHandles.Keys) {\n  1014→        $entry = $WindowHandles[$name]\n  1015→        $handle = if ($entry -is [hashtable]) { $entry.Handle } else { $entry }\n  1016→\n  1017→        if ($handle -and $handle -ne [IntPtr]::Zero) {\n  1018→            # Check if window still exists and is visible\n  1019→            if ([WindowLayoutAPI]::IsWindowVisible($handle)) {\n  1020→                # Restore if minimized\n  1021→                if ([WindowLayoutAPI]::IsIconic($handle)) {\n  1022→                    Write-LayoutLog \"Restoring minimized window: $name\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n  1023→                    [WindowLayoutAPI]::ShowWindow($handle, [WindowLayoutAPI]::SW_RESTORE) | Out-Null\n  1024→                    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 100\n  1025→                }\n  1026→\n  1027→                $validWindows += @{\n  1028→                    Name = $name\n  1029→                    Handle = $handle\n  1030→                }\n  1031→            } else {\n  1032→                Write-LayoutLog \"Filtered invalid/invisible window: $name (handle=$handle)\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n  1033→                $invalidCount++\n  1034→            }\n  1035→        } else {\n  1036→            Write-LayoutLog \"Filtered null/zero handle: $name\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n  1037→            $invalidCount++\n  1038→        }\n  1039→    }\n  1040→\n  1041→    if ($invalidCount -gt 0) {\n  1042→        Write-LayoutLog \"Filtered out $invalidCount invalid window handle(s)\" -Level \"INFO\"\n  1043→    }\n  1044→\n  1045→    $windowCount = $validWindows.Count\n  1046→\n  1047→    if ($windowCount -eq 0) {\n  1048→        Write-LayoutLog \"No valid windows to layout - returning empty\" -Level \"INFO\"\n  1049→        return @()\n  1050→    }\n  1051→\n  1052→    # Get configuration\n  1053→    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n  1054→\n  1055→    # Determine effective mode\n  1056→    $effectiveMode = $Mode\n  1057→    if ($effectiveMode -eq 'Auto') {\n  1058→        # Use config mode, or default to Pillars for 1-4 windows, Quads for 5+\n  1059→        if ($config.Mode -and $config.Mode -ne 'Auto') {\n  1060→            $effectiveMode = $config.Mode\n  1061→        }\n  1062→        else {\n  1063→            $effectiveMode = if ($windowCount -le 4) { 'Pillars' } else { 'Quads' }\n  1064→        }\n  1065→    }\n  1066→\n  1067→    # Get screen topology\n  1068→    $screens = Get-ScreenTopology\n  1069→\n  1070→    if ($screens.Count -eq 0) {\n  1071→        Write-Warning \"No screens detected\"\n  1072→        return @()\n  1073→    }\n  1074→\n  1075→    # Get gap size and windowsOnPrimary from config\n  1076→    $gapSize = if ($config.GapSize) { $config.GapSize } else { 60 }\n  1077→    $windowsOnPrimary = $config.WindowsOnPrimary  # null = Auto (all on primary)\n  1078→\n  1079→    # Calculate layout based on mode\n  1080→    $layout = switch ($effectiveMode) {\n  1081→        'Pillars' {\n  1082→            $maxPillars = if ($config.MaxPillarsPerScreen) { $config.MaxPillarsPerScreen } else { 4 }\n  1083→            Get-PillarsLayout -WindowCount $windowCount -Screens $screens -MaxPillarsPerScreen $maxPillars -GapSize $gapSize -WindowsOnPrimary $windowsOnPrimary\n  1084→        }\n  1085→        'Quads' {\n  1086→            Get-QuadsLayout -WindowCount $windowCount -Screens $screens -GapSize $gapSize -WindowsOnPrimary $windowsOnPrimary\n  1087→        }\n  1088→    }\n  1089→\n  1090→    if (-not $layout -or $layout.Count -eq 0) {\n  1091→        Write-Warning \"Layout calculation returned no positions\"\n  1092→        return @()\n  1093→    }\n  1094→\n  1095→    # Sort windows by name to ensure consistent ordering (Matrix-1 before Matrix-2, etc.)\n  1096→    $sortedWindows = $validWindows | Sort-Object { $_.Name }\n  1097→\n  1098→    # Build result with handle-to-position mapping\n  1099→    $result = @()\n  1100→    for ($i = 0; $i -lt [Math]::Min($sortedWindows.Count, $layout.Count); $i++) {\n  1101→        $window = $sortedWindows[$i]\n  1102→        $position = $layout[$i]\n  1103→\n  1104→        $result += @{\n  1105→            Name = $window.Name\n  1106→            Handle = $window.Handle\n  1107→            X = $position.X\n  1108→            Y = $position.Y\n  1109→            Width = $position.Width\n  1110→            Height = $position.Height\n  1111→            ScreenIndex = $position.ScreenIndex\n  1112→        }\n  1113→    }\n  1114→\n  1115→    # Apply layout if not dry run\n  1116→    if (-not $DryRun) {\n  1117→        foreach ($item in $result) {\n  1118→            try {\n  1119→                [WindowLayoutAPI]::SetWindowPos(\n  1120→                    $item.Handle,\n  1121→                    [IntPtr]::Zero,\n  1122→                    $item.X,\n  1123→                    $item.Y,\n  1124→                    $item.Width,\n  1125→                    $item.Height,\n  1126→                    ([WindowLayoutAPI]::SWP_NOZORDER -bor [WindowLayoutAPI]::SWP_SHOWWINDOW)\n  1127→                ) | Out-Null\n  1128→\n  1129→                Write-Verbose \"Positioned $($item.Name) at ($($item.X), $($item.Y)) size $($item.Width)x$($item.Height)\"\n  1130→            }\n  1131→            catch {\n  1132→                Write-Warning \"Failed to position $($item.Name): $_\"\n  1133→            }\n  1134→        }\n  1135→    }\n  1136→\n  1137→    return $result\n  1138→}\n  1139→\n  1140→<#\n  1141→.SYNOPSIS\n  1142→    Get the calculated layout for a given window count without window handles.\n  1143→\n  1144→.DESCRIPTION\n  1145→    Utility function to preview layout calculations. Useful for testing\n  1146→    and displaying layout information in the UI.\n  1147→\n  1148→.PARAMETER WindowCount\n  1149→    Number of windows to calculate layout for\n  1150→\n  1151→.PARAMETER Mode\n  1152→    Layout strategy: 'Pillars' or 'Quads'\n  1153→\n  1154→.PARAMETER Screens\n  1155→    Optional array of screen objects. If not provided, uses Get-ScreenTopology\n  1156→\n  1157→.OUTPUTS\n  1158→    Array of @{ X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex, WindowIndex }\n  1159→\n  1160→.EXAMPLE\n  1161→    Get-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowCount 4 -Mode 'Pillars'\n  1162→    # Returns 4 rectangle positions in pillars layout\n  1163→#>\n  1164→function Get-MatrixWindowLayout {\n  1165→    param(\n  1166→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n  1167→        [int]$WindowCount,\n  1168→\n  1169→        [ValidateSet('Pillars', 'Quads')]\n  1170→        [string]$Mode = 'Pillars',\n  1171→\n  1172→        [array]$Screens\n  1173→    )\n  1174→\n  1175→    if (-not $Screens -or $Screens.Count -eq 0) {\n  1176→        $Screens = Get-ScreenTopology\n  1177→    }\n  1178→\n  1179→    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n  1180→    $gapSize = if ($config.GapSize) { $config.GapSize } else { 60 }\n  1181→\n  1182→    switch ($Mode) {\n  1183→        'Pillars' {\n  1184→            $maxPillars = if ($config.MaxPillarsPerScreen) { $config.MaxPillarsPerScreen } else { 4 }\n  1185→            Get-PillarsLayout -WindowCount $WindowCount -Screens $Screens -MaxPillarsPerScreen $maxPillars -GapSize $gapSize\n  1186→        }\n  1187→        'Quads' {\n  1188→            Get-QuadsLayout -WindowCount $WindowCount -Screens $Screens -GapSize $gapSize\n  1189→        }\n  1190→    }\n  1191→}\n  1192→\n  1193→# --- POSITION TRACKING FOR DRAG DETECTION ---\n  1194→\n  1195→# Module-level state for position tracking\n  1196→$script:LastKnownPositions = @{}\n  1197→$script:DragThreshold = 50  # Minimum pixels of movement to consider a drag\n  1198→\n  1199→<#\n  1200→.SYNOPSIS\n  1201→    Get current window positions for a set of window handles.\n  1202→\n  1203→.DESCRIPTION\n  1204→    Uses GetWindowRect API to retrieve current X, Y, Width, Height for each window.\n  1205→    Returns a hashtable keyed by handle (as string) with position data.\n  1206→\n  1207→.PARAMETER WindowHandles\n  1208→    Array of window handle IntPtrs\n  1209→\n  1210→.OUTPUTS\n  1211→    Hashtable @{ \"handle\" = @{ X, Y, Width, Height } }\n  1212→\n  1213→.EXAMPLE\n  1214→    $positions = Get-WindowPositions -WindowHandles @($hwnd1, $hwnd2)\n  1215→#>\n  1216→function Get-WindowPositions {\n  1217→    param(\n  1218→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n  1219→        [array]$WindowHandles\n  1220→    )\n  1221→\n  1222→    $positions = @{}\n  1223→\n  1224→    foreach ($handle in $WindowHandles) {\n  1225→        if (-not $handle -or $handle -eq [IntPtr]::Zero) { continue }\n  1226→\n  1227→        try {\n  1228→            $rect = New-Object WindowLayoutAPI+RECT\n  1229→            if ([WindowLayoutAPI]::GetWindowRect($handle, [ref]$rect)) {\n  1230→                $positions[$handle.ToString()] = @{\n  1231→                    X = $rect.Left\n  1232→                    Y = $rect.Top\n  1233→                    Width = $rect.Right - $rect.Left\n  1234→                    Height = $rect.Bottom - $rect.Top\n  1235→                }\n  1236→            }\n  1237→        }\n  1238→        catch {\n  1239→            Write-LayoutLog \"Failed to get position for handle $handle : $_\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n  1240→        }\n  1241→    }\n  1242→\n  1243→    return $positions\n  1244→}\n  1245→\n  1246→<#\n  1247→.SYNOPSIS\n  1248→    Initialize position tracking for a set of windows.\n  1249→\n  1250→.DESCRIPTION\n  1251→    Captures current window positions and stores them as the baseline\n  1252→    for drag detection. Should be called when windows are first positioned.\n  1253→\n  1254→.PARAMETER WindowHandles\n  1255→    Array of window handle IntPtrs to track\n  1256→\n  1257→.EXAMPLE\n  1258→ [array]$WindowHandles\n  1264→    )\n  1265→\n  1266→    $script:LastKnownPositions = Get-WindowPositions -WindowHandles $WindowHandles\n  1267→    Write-LayoutLog \"Initialized position tracking for $($WindowHandles.Count) windows\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n  1268→}\n  1269→\n  1270→<#\n  1271→.SYNOPSIS\n  1272→    Detect if any window has been dragged since last check.\n  1273→\n  1274→.DESCRIPTION\n  1275→    Compares current window positions to last known positions.\n  1276→    Returns $true if any window has moved more than the drag threshold.\n  1277→\n  1278→.PARAMETER WindowHandles\n  1279→ @($hwnd1, $hwnd2)) {\n  1286→        # Window was dragged, reposition all\n  1287→    }\n  1288→#>\n  1289→function Test-WindowDragDetected {\n  1290→    param(\n  1291→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n  1292→        [array]$WindowHandles\n  1293→    )\n  1294→\n  1295→    if ($script:LastKnownPositions.Count -eq 0) {\n  1296→        # No baseline - initialize and return false\n  1297→        Initialize-PositionTracking -WindowHandles $WindowHandles\n  1298→        return $false\n  1299→    }\n  1300→\n  1301→    $currentPositions = Get-WindowPositions -WindowHandles $WindowHandles\n  1302→\n  1303→    foreach ($handleKey in $currentPositions.Keys) {\n  1304→        if (-not $script:LastKnownPositions.ContainsKey($handleKey)) {\n  1305→            # New window appeared - treat as drag event\n  1306→            Write-LayoutLog \"New window detected: $handleKey\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n  1307→            return $true\n  1308→        }\n  1309→\n  1310→        $current = $currentPositions[$handleKey]\n  1311→        $last = $script:LastKnownPositions[$handleKey]\n  1312→\n  1313→        $deltaX = [Math]::Abs($current.X - $last.X)\n  1314→        $deltaY = [Math]::Abs($current.Y - $last.Y)\n  1315→\n  1316→        if ($deltaX -gt $script:DragThreshold -or $deltaY -gt $script:DragThreshold) {\n  1317→            Write-LayoutLog \"Drag detected on $handleKey : delta ($deltaX, $deltaY)\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n  1318→            return $true\n  1319→        }\n  1320→    }\n  1321→\n  1322→    return $false\n  1323→}\n  1324→\n  1325→<#\n 1331→\n  1332→.PARAMETER WindowHandles\n  1333→    Array of window handle IntPtrs\n  1334→\n  1335→.EXAMPLE\n  1336→    Update-PositionTracking -WindowHandles @($hwnd1, $hwnd2)\n  1337→#>\n  1338→function Update-PositionTracking {\n  1339→    param(\n  1340→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n  1341→        [array]$WindowHandles\n  1342→    )\n  1343→\n  1344→    $script:LastKnownPositions = Get-WindowPositions -WindowHandles $WindowHandles\n  1345→    Write-LayoutLog \"Updated position tracking for $($WindowHandles.Count) windows\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n  1346→}\n  1347→\n  1348→# --- POSITION PERSISTENCE ---\n  1349→\n  1350→<#\n  1351→.SYNOPSIS\n  1352→    Save window positions to matrix_state.json for later restoration.\n  1353→\n  1354→.DESCRIPTION\n  1355→    Persists current position for $shaderName : ($($rect.Left), $($rect.Top)) $($rect.Right - $rect.Left)x$($rect.Bottom - $rect.Top)\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n  1405→                }\n  1406→            }\n  1407→            catch {\n  1408→                Write-LayoutLog \"Failed to get position for slot $($win.Slot): $_\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n  1409→            }\n  1410→        }\n  1411→\n  1412→        # Update state\n  1413→        $state.windowPositions = $positions\n  1414→        $state.positionsSavedAt = (Get-Date).ToString(\"o\")\n  1415→\n  1416→        # Atomic write\n  1417→        $stateJson = $state | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10 -ErrorAction Stop\n  1418→        $tempFile = [System.IO.Path]::GetTempFileName()\n  1419→        $stateJson | Out-File -FilePath $tempFile -Encoding UTF8 -ErrorAction Stop\n  1420→        Move-Item -Path $tempFile -Destination $stateFilePath -Force -ErrorAction Stop\n  1421→\n  1422→        Write-LayoutLog \"Saved positions for $($positions.Count) windows\" -Level \"INFO\"\n  1423→        return $true\n  1424→    }\n  1425→    catch {\n  1426→        Write-LayoutLog \"Failed to save window positions: $_\" -Level \"ERROR\"\n  1427→        return $false\n  1428→    }\n  1429→}\n  1430→\n  1431→<#\n  1432→.SYNOPSIS\n  1433→    Restore window positions from matrix_state.json.\n  1434→\n  1435→.DESCRIPTION\n  1436→    Reads saved positions and applies them to windows.\n  1437→    Returns $true if positions were restored, $false if no saved positions or error.\n  1438→\n  1439→.PARAMETER WindowInfo\n  1440→    Array of @{ Handle, Slot } objects from Get-MatrixWindowInfo\n  1441→\n  1442→.OUTPUTS\n  1443→    $true if restored, $false otherwise\n  1444→\n  1445→.EXAMPLE\n  1446→    $windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowInfo\n  1447→    if (Restore-WindowPositions -WindowInfo $windowInfo) {\n  1448→        Write-Host \"Restored saved positions\"\n  1449→    } else {\n  1450→        # Fall back to layout engine\n  1451→ 1459→\n  1460→    $stateFilePath = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\Documents\\Matrix\\matrix_state.json\"\n  1461→\n  1462→    try {\n  1463→        if (-not (Test-Path $stateFilePath)) {\n  1464→            Write-LayoutLog \"No state file for position restore\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n  1465→            return $false\n  1466→        }\n  1467→\n  1468→        $stateJson = Get-Content -Path $stateFilePath -Raw -ErrorAction Stop\n  1469→        $state = $stateJson | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop\n  1470→\n  1471→        if (-not $state.windowPositions) {\n  1472→            Write-LayoutLog \"No saved positions in state file\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n  1473→            return $false\n  1474→        }\n  1475→\n  1476→        $restoredCount = 0\n  1477→        foreach ($win in $WindowInfo) {\n  1478→            $shaderName = \"Matrix-$($win.Slot)\"\n  1479→\n  1480→            # Check if we have saved position for this shader\n  1481→            $savedPos = $state.windowPositions.$shaderName\n  1482→ ([WindowLayoutAPI]::SWP_NOZORDER -bor [WindowLayoutAPI]::SWP_SHOWWINDOW)\n  1496→                ) | Out-Null\n  1497→\n  1498→                Write-LayoutLog \"Restored position for $shaderName : ($($savedPos.x), $($savedPos.y)) $($savedPos.width)x$($savedPos.height)\" -Level \"DEBUG\"\n  1499→                $restoredCount++\n  1500→            }\n  1501→            catch {\n  1502→
