| ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop\n\n        # Check if window title contains any profile name with a shader override\n        foreach ($profile in $settings.profiles.list) {\n            if ($profile.name -and $windowTitle -like \"*$($profile.name)*\") {\n                if ($profile.'experimental.pixelShaderPath' -match \"Matrix-(\\d+)\\.hlsl\") {\n                    return [int]$Matches[1]\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        # Fallback to profiles.defaults\n        $defaultShader = $settings.profiles.defaults.'experimental.pixelShaderPath'\n        if ($defaultShader -match \"Matrix-(\\d+)\\.hlsl\") {\n            return [int]$Matches[1]\n        }\n    } catch { }\n    return $null\n}\n\nfunction Get-MatrixWindowInfo {\n    # Returns array of @{Handle, Title, Slot, ShaderPath} for Matrix windows ONLY\n    # Uses title matching to detect Matrix-N windows\n    Write-Log \"Detecting Matrix windows...\" \"DETECT\"\n    $windows = Get-MatrixWindows\n    Write-Log \"Found $($windows.Count) terminal windows\" \"DETECT\"\n    $result = @()\n\n    foreach ($win in $windows) {\n        $slot = $null\n        $shaderFile = $null\n\n        # Title matching (Matrix-N in title)\n        if ($win.Value -match \"Matrix-(\\d+)\") {\n            $slot = [int]$Matches[1]\n            $shaderFile = \"Matrix-$slot.hlsl\"\n            Write-Log \"  Title match: handle=$($win.Key) -> Slot $slot\" \"DETECT\"\n        }\n        # No Matrix profile detected - skip this window\n        else {\n            Write-Log \"  Skipping non-Matrix window: handle=$($win.Key) title='$($win.Value)'\" \"DETECT\"\n            continue\n        }\n\n        $result += @{\n            Handle = $win.Key\n            Title = $win.Value\n            Slot = $slot\n            ShaderFile = $shaderFile\n            ShaderPath = \"$shadersDir\\$shaderFile\"\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Update registry with detected mappings\n    $registry = @{}\n    foreach ($w in $result) {\n        $registry[$w.Handle.ToString()] = $w.ShaderFile\n    }\n    try {\n        $registry | ConvertTo-Json | Set-Content $windowRegistryPath -Encoding UTF8\n    } catch { }\n\n    $sorted = $result | Sort-Object { $_.Slot }\n    Write-Log \"Detected $($sorted.Count) Matrix windows\" \"DETECT\"\n    foreach ($w in $sorted) {\n        Write-Log \"  Slot $($w.Slot): handle=$($w.Handle) title='$($w.Title)'\" \"DETECT\"\n    }\n    return $sorted\n}\n\nfunction Apply-WindowTransparency {\n    # Apply transparency via Windows Terminal profile settings (background only, not text/effects)\n    # This modifies settings.json and Windows Terminal hot-reloads\n    try {\n        $content = Get-Content $wtSettingsPath -Raw -ErrorAction Stop\n        $settings = $content | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop\n\n        # Apply to profiles.defaults (affects windows using default profile)\n        if ($script:transparency) {\n            $settings.profiles.defaults | Add-Member -NotePropertyName 'opacity' -NotePropertyValue $script:opacity -Force\n        } else {\n            $settings.profiles.defaults.PSObject.Properties.Remove('opacity')\n        }\n\n        # Apply to ALL Matrix-N profiles\n        for ($i = 0; $i -lt $settings.profiles.list.Count; $i++) {\n            $profileName = $settings.profiles.list[$i].name\n            if ($profileName -match \"^Matrix-\\d+$\") {\n                if ($script:transparency) {\n                    $settings.profiles.list[$i] | Add-Member -NotePropertyName 'opacity' -NotePropertyValue $script:opacity -Force\n                } else {\n                    $settings.profiles.list[$i].PSObject.Properties.Remove('opacity')\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        # Safe atomic write\n        $json = $settings | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10\n        $tempPath = \"$wtSettingsPath.tmp\"\n        [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($tempPath, $json, [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8)\n        Move-Item $tempPath $wtSettingsPath -Force -ErrorAction Stop\n    }\n    catch { }\n}\n\nfunction Wait-ForMatrixWindow([string]$profileName, [int]$timeoutMs = 5000) {\n    # Poll for window with title containing profileName (e.g., \"Matrix-1\")\n    # Returns $true if found, $false if timeout\n    # Uses fast title-only search (no process lookup) for speed\n    $pollInterval = 100\n    $startTime = Get-Date\n\n    while ($true) {\n        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $pollInterval\n\n        # Strict timeout check\n        if (((Get-Date) - $startTime).TotalMilliseconds -ge $timeoutMs) {\n            return $false\n        }\n\n        # Fast check - just look for window title matching profile name\n        $matches = [WindowAPI]::FindWindowsByPattern($profileName)\n        if ($matches.Count -gt 0) {\n            return $true\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfunction Launch-MatrixWindows([int]$count) {\n    Write-Log \"Launch requested: count=$count\" \"LAUNCH\"\n    $existingSlots = Get-ExistingSlots\n    $openSlots = Get-OpenMatrixSlots\n    Write-Log \"Existing slots: $($existingSlots -join ',') Open: $($openSlots -join ',')\" \"LAUNCH\"\n\n    # Find available slots (exist but not currently open)\n    $availableSlots = $existingSlots | Where-Object { $_ -notin $openSlots }\n\n    if ($availableSlots.Count -eq 0) {\n        Write-Log \"No available slots to launch\" \"LAUNCH\"\n        Write-Host \"\"\n        Write-Host \" All Matrix windows are already open!\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n        Start-Sleep -Seconds 2\n        return\n    }\n\n    $numWindows = [Math]::Min($count, $availableSlots.Count)\n    $slotsToLaunch = $availableSlots | Select-Object -First $numWindows\n    Write-Log \"Launching slots: $($slotsToLaunch -join ',')\" \"LAUNCH\"\n\n    # Save shaders for slots we're launching\n    foreach ($slot in $slotsToLaunch) {\n        $cfg = Load-Shader $slot\n        Save-Shader $slot $cfg\n    }\n\n    Write-Host \"\"\n    Write-Host \" Launching $numWindows Matrix window(s)...\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n    Write-Host \" Open: [$($openSlots -join ', ')] | Launching: [$($slotsToLaunch -join ', ')]\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n\n    foreach ($slot in $slotsToLaunch) {\n        $pname = \"Matrix-$slot\"\n        Write-Host \"   Waiting for $pname...\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray -NoNewline\n        Start-Process wt -ArgumentList \"-p `\"$pname`\"\"\n\n        if (Wait-ForMatrixWindow $pname) {\n            Write-Log \"Window $pname launched successfully\" \"LAUNCH\"\n            Write-Host \" OK\" -ForegroundColor Green\n        } else {\n            Write-Log \"Window $pname TIMEOUT after 5s\" \"LAUNCH\"\n            Write-Host \" TIMEOUT (5s)\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Position ALL open Matrix windows (existing + new)\n    Write-Host \" Positioning windows...\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n    Position-MatrixWindows\n\n    # Start background monitor for drag-snap (if not already running)\n    $monitorScript = \"$PSScriptRoot\\matrix_monitor.ps1\"\n    if (Test-Path $monitorScript) {\n        Start-Process powershell -ArgumentList \"-NoProfile -WindowStyle Hidden -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File `\"$monitorScript`\"\" -WindowStyle Hidden\n    }\n\n    Write-Host \" THE MATRIX HAS YOU.\" -ForegroundColor Green\n    Start-Sleep -Seconds 2\n}\n\nfunction UI {\n    Clear-Host\n    $windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowInfo  # Get ALL Windows Terminal windows except Redpill\n    $dirtyMark = if ($dirty) { \"*\" } else { \" \" }\n\n    Write-Host \"\"\n    Write-Host \" RED PILL$dirtyMark- Tab $currentSlot\" -ForegroundColor Red\n    Write-Host \"\"\n\n    # Tab selector - shows ALL Windows Terminal windows with shader effects\n    Write-Host \" TABS: \" -NoNewline\n    if ($windowInfo.Count -eq 0) {\n        Write-Host \"(no Matrix windows detected)\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray -NoNewline\n    } else {\n        foreach ($winInfo in $windowInfo) {\n            $slot = $winInfo.Slot\n            $title = $winInfo.Title\n            # For Matrix-N windows, load their shader config. For others, use defaults\n            if ($slot -lt 100) {\n                $cfg = Load-Shader $slot\n                $displayName = \"$slot\"\n            } else {\n                $cfg = $defaults.Clone()\n                # Abbreviate long titles (first 8 chars)\n                if ($title.Length -gt 8) {\n                    $displayName = $title.Substring(0, 8) + \"..\"\n                } else {\n                    $displayName = $title\n                }\n            }\n            if ($slot -eq $currentSlot) {\n                Write-Host \"[$displayName]$(Swatch $cfg.R $cfg.G $cfg.B 1)\" -NoNewline -ForegroundColor Yellow\n            } else {\n                Write-Host \" $displayName $(Swatch $cfg.R $cfg.G $cfg.B 1)\" -NoNewline -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n            }\n            Write-Host \" \" -NoNewline\n        }\n    }\n    Write-Host \"\"\n    Write-Host \" [TAB] next tab\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    Write-Host \"\"\n\n    # Color section\n    Write-Host \" COLOR PRESETS\" -ForegroundColor White\n    Write-Host \" [1]$(Swatch 0 1 0.3 1)Green [2]$(Swatch 0 0.6 1 1)Cyan [3]$(Swatch 1 0.1 0.1 1)Red [4]$(Swatch 0.7 0 1 1)Purple [5]$(Swatch 1 0.7 0 1)Gold [6]$(Swatch 0 0.9 0.9 1)Teal\"\n    Write-Host \"\"\n\n    # Current color with sliders\n    Write-Host \" CURRENT $(Swatch $s.R $s.G $s.B 3)\" -ForegroundColor White\n    Write-Host \" [Q/W] Red   $($s.R.PadLeft(4)) $(Bar $s.R 0 1 15)\"\n    Write-Host \" [A/S] Green $($s.G.PadLeft(4)) $(Bar $s.G 0 1 15)\"\n    Write-Host \" [Z/X] Blue  $($s.B.PadLeft(4)) $(Bar $s.B 0 1 15)\"\n    Write-Host \"\"\n\n    # Effects\n    Write-Host \" RAIN EFFECTS\" -ForegroundColor White\n    Write-Host \" [E/R] Speed   $($s.Speed.PadLeft(4)) $(Bar $s.Speed 0.1 3 15)\"\n    Write-Host \" [D/F] Glow    $($s.Glow.PadLeft(4)) $(Bar $s.Glow 0.2 3 15)\"\n    Write-Host \" [C/V] Width   $($s.Width.PadLeft(4)) $(Bar $s.Width 6 20 15)\"\n    Write-Host \" [T/Y] Trail   $($s.Trail.PadLeft(4)) $(Bar $s.Trail 4 15 15)\"\n    Write-Host \" [G/H] Density $($s.Dens.PadLeft(4)) $(Bar $s.Dens 0.2 1 15)\"\n    Write-Host \"\"\n\n    # Layers\n    $l1 = if($s.L1 -eq \"1.0\"){\"ON \"}else{\"off\"}\n    $l2 = if($s.L2 -eq \"1.0\"){\"ON \"}else{\"off\"}\n    $l3 = if($s.L3 -eq \"1.0\"){\"ON \"}else{\"off\"}\n    $l1c = if($s.L1 -eq \"1.0\"){\"Green\"}else{\"DarkGray\"}\n    $l2c = if($s.L2 -eq \"1.0\"){\"Green\"}else{\"DarkGray\"}\n    $l3c = if($s.L3 -eq \"1.0\"){\"Green\"}else{\"DarkGray\"}\n    Write-Host \" LAYERS\" -ForegroundColor White\n    Write-Host \" [7] Far: \" -NoNewline; Write-Host $l1 -ForegroundColor $l1c -NoNewline\n    Write-Host \"  [8] Mid: \" -NoNewline; Write-Host $l2 -ForegroundColor $l2c -NoNewline\n    Write-Host \"  [9] Near: \" -NoNewline; Write-Host $l3 -ForegroundColor $l3c\n    Write-Host \"\"\n\n    # Terminal Effects (transparency and layout)\n    Write-Host \" WINDOW EFFECTS\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n    $transStatus = if($transparency){\"ON \"}else{\"off\"}\n    $transColor = if($transparency){\"Cyan\"}else{\"DarkGray\"}\n    Write-Host \" [B] Transparency:  \" -NoNewline; Write-Host $transStatus -ForegroundColor $transColor -NoNewline\n    Write-Host \"  (toggles & applies)\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    if ($transparency) {\n        Write-Host \" [K/l] Opacity:     $($opacity.ToString().PadLeft(3))% $(Bar $opacity 0 100 15)\"\n    }\n    # Layout mode display\n    $layoutConfig = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n    $layoutMode = if ($layoutConfig.Mode) { $layoutConfig.Mode } else { 'Pillars' }\n    $layoutColor = if ($layoutMode -eq 'Pillars') { \"Yellow\" } else { \"Magenta\" }\n    Write-Host \" [Shift+L] Layout:  \" -NoNewline; Write-Host $layoutMode -ForegroundColor $layoutColor -NoNewline\n    Write-Host \"  (Pillars=columns, Quads=2x2)\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n\n    # Windows on Primary display\n    $screens = Get-ScreenTopology\n    if ($screens.Count -gt 1) {\n        $windowsOnPrimary = $layoutConfig.WindowsOnPrimary\n        if ($null -eq $windowsOnPrimary -or $windowsOnPrimary -lt 0) {\n            $primaryDisplay = \"Auto\"\n            $primaryColor = \"Cyan\"\n        } else {\n            $primaryDisplay = \"$windowsOnPrimary\"\n            $primaryColor = \"Yellow\"\n        }\n        Write-Host \" [</>] Primary:     \" -NoNewline; Write-Host $primaryDisplay -ForegroundColor $primaryColor -NoNewline\n        Write-Host \"  (windows on primary monitor)  [)] Auto\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    }\n    Write-Host \"\"\n\n    # Launch section\n    Write-Host \" LAUNCH\" -ForegroundColor Magenta\n    $existingSlots = Get-ExistingSlots\n    $openSlots = Get-OpenMatrixSlots\n    $availableSlots = $existingSlots | Where-Object { $_ -notin $openSlots }\n    $availableCount = $availableSlots.Count\n\n    # Show open/available status\n    $openStr = if ($openSlots.Count -gt 0) { $openSlots -join ',' } else { \"none\" }\n    $availStr = if ($availableCount -gt 0) { $availableSlots -join ',' } else { \"none\" }\n    Write-Host \" Open: \" -NoNewline -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    Write-Host $openStr -NoNewline -ForegroundColor Green\n    Write-Host \" | Available: \" -NoNewline -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    Write-Host $availStr -ForegroundColor Cyan\n\n    $launchStatus = if($launchCount -gt 0){\"$launchCount window(s)\"}else{\"disabled\"}\n    $launchColor = if($launchCount -gt 0){\"Magenta\"}else{\"DarkGray\"}\n    Write-Host \" [-/+] Count: \" -NoNewline; Write-Host $launchStatus -ForegroundColor $launchColor -NoNewline\n    Write-Host \" (max: $availableCount)\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    Write-Host \"\"\n\n    # Footer\n    $enterAction = if($launchCount -gt 0){\"[ENTER] Launch $launchCount window(s)\"}else{\"[ENTER] (set count first)\"}\n    $enterColor = if($launchCount -gt 0){\"Yellow\"}else{\"DarkGray\"}\n    Write-Host \" $enterAction  \" -ForegroundColor $enterColor -NoNewline\n    Write-Host \"[P] Save shader\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n    Write-Host \" [0] Reset  [ESC] Quit\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    Write-Host \"\"\n    Write-Host \" Shader changes apply automatically when saved (hot-reload)\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n}\n\n# Check for existing shaders\n$existingSlots = Get-ExistingSlots\nif ($existingSlots.Count -eq 0) {\n    Write-Host \"\"\n    Write-Host \" No Matrix tabs found.\" -ForegroundColor Red\n    Write-Host \" Run 'wakeupneo' first to create some.\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n    Write-Host \"\"\n    exit 1\n}\n\n# Clean stale window registry entries and populate with current windows\nClean-WindowRegistry\nPopulate-WindowRegistry\n\n# Load first OPEN slot (or first existing if none open)\n$openSlots = Get-OpenMatrixSlots\nif ($openSlots.Count -gt 0) {\n    $currentSlot = $openSlots[0]\n} else {\n    $currentSlot = $existingSlots[0]  # Fallback if no windows open yet\n}\n$s = Load-Shader $currentSlot\nLoad-TerminalEffects $currentSlot\n\n# Drag detection is handled by matrix_monitor.ps1 background process\n# Redpill uses simple blocking input for responsive UI\n\n[Console]::CursorVisible = $false\ntry {\n    while ($true) {\n        UI\n        $key = $Host.UI.RawUI.ReadKey(\"NoEcho,IncludeKeyDown\")\n        $k = $key.Character\n        $vk = $key.VirtualKeyCode\n\n        # Tab key (VK 9) to switch between OPEN windows only\n        if ($vk -eq 9) {\n            # Auto-save before switching tabs\n            if ($dirty) {\n                Save-Shader $currentSlot $s\n                $dirty = $false\n            }\n            $openSlots = Get-OpenMatrixSlots\n            if ($openSlots.Count -gt 0) {\n                $idx = [array]::IndexOf($openSlots, $currentSlot)\n                if ($idx -lt 0) { $idx = 0 }  # If current slot closed, go to first\n                $idx = ($idx + 1) % $openSlots.Count\n                $currentSlot = $openSlots[$idx]\n                $s = Load-Shader $currentSlot\n                Load-TerminalEffects $currentSlot\n                $dirty = $false\n            }\n        }\n        # Enter key (VK 13) to launch windows (only if launchCount > 0)\n        elseif ($vk -eq 13) {\n            if ($launchCount -gt 0) {\n                Save-Shader $currentSlot $s\n                $dirty = $false\n                Launch-MatrixWindows $launchCount\n            }\n        }\n        # Escape key (VK 27) to quit\n        elseif ($vk -eq 27) {\n            # Save current window setup for next Blue Pill run\n            Save-CurrentState\n\n            # Save window positions for exact restoration (like Chrome)\n            $windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowInfo\n            if ($windowInfo.Count -gt 0) {\n                Save-WindowPositions -WindowInfo $windowInfo\n            }\n\n            return\n        }\n        else {\n            # Handle Shift+L (uppercase L) for layout mode BEFORE normalizing\n            # This preserves case-sensitivity for layout toggle vs opacity control\n            if ($k -ceq 'L') {\n                # Cycle layout mode: Pillars -> Quads -> Pillars\n                $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n                $newMode = if ($config.Mode -eq 'Pillars') { 'Quads' } else { 'Pillars' }\n                $config.Mode = $newMode\n                Set-MatrixLayoutConfig -Config $config\n                Position-MatrixWindows\n                Write-Host \"\"\n                Write-Host \" Layout mode: $newMode\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n                Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 800\n                continue\n            }\n\n            # Normalize letter keys to lowercase for case-insensitive handling\n            $key = if ($k -match '^[A-Za-z]$') { [char]::ToLower($k) } else { $k }\n\n            switch ($key) {\n                # Color presets (1-6)\n                '1' { $s.R=\"0.0\"; $s.G=\"1.0\"; $s.B=\"0.3\"; $dirty=$true }\n                '2' { $s.R=\"0.0\"; $s.G=\"0.6\"; $s.B=\"1.0\"; $dirty=$true }\n                '3' { $s.R=\"1.0\"; $s.G=\"0.1\"; $s.B=\"0.1\"; $dirty=$true }\n                '4' { $s.R=\"0.7\"; $s.G=\"0.0\"; $s.B=\"1.0\"; $dirty=$true }\n                '5' { $s.R=\"1.0\"; $s.G=\"0.7\"; $s.B=\"0.0\"; $dirty=$true }\n                '6' { $s.R=\"0.0\"; $s.G=\"0.9\"; $s.B=\"0.9\"; $dirty=$true }\n\n                # RGB controls (Q/W, A/S, Z/X)\n                'q' { Adj 'R' -0.05 0 1 }\n                'w' { Adj 'R' 0.05 0 1 }\n                'a' { Adj 'G' -0.05 0 1 }\n                's' { Adj 'G' 0.05 0 1 }\n                'z' { Adj 'B' -0.05 0 1 }\n                'x' { Adj 'B' 0.05 0 1 }\n\n                # Effects (paired keys for -/+)\n                'e' { Adj 'Speed' -0.1 0.1 3 }\n                'r' { Adj 'Speed' 0.1 0.1 3 }\n                'd' { Adj 'Glow' -0.1 0.2 3 }\n                'f' { Adj 'Glow' 0.1 0.2 3 }\n                'c' { Adj 'Width' -1 6 20 }\n                'v' { Adj 'Width' 1 6 20 }\n                't' { Adj 'Trail' -0.5 4 15 }\n                'y' { Adj 'Trail' 0.5 4 15 }\n                'g' { Adj 'Dens' -0.1 0.2 1 }\n                'h' { Adj 'Dens' 0.1 0.2 1 }\n\n                # Layers (7/8/9)\n                '7' { $s.L1 = if($s.L1 -eq \"1.0\"){\"0.0\"}else{\"1.0\"}; $dirty=$true }\n                '8' { $s.L2 = if($s.L2 -eq \"1.0\"){\"0.0\"}else{\"1.0\"}; $dirty=$true }\n                '9' { $s.L3 = if($s.L3 -eq \"1.0\"){\"0.0\"}else{\"1.0\"}; $dirty=$true }\n\n                # Window transparency\n                'b' { $script:transparency = -not $transparency; Apply-WindowTransparency }\n                'k' { if ($transparency -and $opacity -gt 0) { $script:opacity = $opacity - 5; Apply-WindowTransparency } }\n                'l' { if ($transparency -and $opacity -lt 100) { $script:opacity = $opacity + 5; Apply-WindowTransparency } }\n\n                # Launch count controls\n                '-' { if ($launchCount -gt 0) { $script:launchCount = $launchCount - 1 } }\n                '+' {\n                    $existingSlots = Get-ExistingSlots\n                    $openSlots = Get-OpenMatrixSlots\n                    $availableCount = ($existingSlots | Where-Object { $_ -notin $openSlots }).Count\n                    if ($launchCount -lt $availableCount) { $script:launchCount = $launchCount + 1 }\n                }\n                '=' {\n                    $existingSlots = Get-ExistingSlots\n                    $openSlots = Get-OpenMatrixSlots\n                    $availableCount = ($existingSlots | Where-Object { $_ -notin $openSlots }).Count\n                    if ($launchCount -lt $availableCount) { $script:launchCount = $launchCount + 1 }\n                }\n\n                # Reset\n                '0' { $s = $defaults.Clone(); $dirty=$true }\n\n                # Save shader\n                'p' {\n                    Save-Shader $currentSlot $s\n                    $dirty = $false\n                    Write-Host \"\"\n                    Write-Host \" Shader saved! Changes apply automatically.\" -ForegroundColor Green\n                    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 1200\n                }\n\n                # Windows on Primary controls (< and > keys = comma and period)\n                ',' {\n                    # Decrease windows on primary\n                    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n                    $screens = Get-ScreenTopology\n                    if ($screens.Count -gt 1) {\n                        $current = $config.WindowsOnPrimary\n                        if ($null -eq $current -or $current -lt 0) {\n                            # Auto mode -> switch to one less than total windows\n                            $openCount = (Get-OpenMatrixSlots).Count\n                            $config.WindowsOnPrimary = [Math]::Max(0, $openCount - 1)\n                        } elseif ($current -gt 0) {\n                            $config.WindowsOnPrimary = $current - 1\n                        }\n                        Set-MatrixLayoutConfig -Config $config\n                        Position-MatrixWindows\n                        Write-Host \"\"\n                        Write-Host \" Primary: $($config.WindowsOnPrimary) windows\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n                        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500\n                    }\n                }\n                '.' {\n                    # Increase windows on primary\n                    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n                    $screens = Get-ScreenTopology\n                    if ($screens.Count -gt 1) {\n                        $current = $config.WindowsOnPrimary\n                        $openCount = (Get-OpenMatrixSlots).Count\n                        if ($null -eq $current -or $current -lt 0) {\n                            # Already auto (all on primary), can't increase more\n                        } elseif ($current -lt $openCount) {\n                            $config.WindowsOnPrimary = $current + 1\n                            Set-MatrixLayoutConfig -Config $config\n                            Position-MatrixWindows\n                            Write-Host \"\"\n                            Write-Host \" Primary: $($config.WindowsOnPrimary) windows\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n                            Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                ')' {\n                    # Reset to Auto (Shift+0)\n                    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n                    $screens = Get-ScreenTopology\n                    if ($screens.Count -gt 1) {\n                        $config.WindowsOnPrimary = $null\n                        Set-MatrixLayoutConfig -Config $config\n                        Position-MatrixWindows\n                        Write-Host \"\"\n                        Write-Host \" Primary: Auto (all windows)\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n                        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n} finally {\n    [Console]::CursorVisible = $true\n}\n","numLines":1055,"startLine":1,"totalLines":1055}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"da17eabe-313f-4f0e-b79c-df742ab9fdef"}

{"parentUuid":"ffb046d0-40e7-465c-bdc3-5f828254d7b0","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX","sessionId":"7950de6a-373b-44cb-ac45-a9b755daa3bc","version":"2.1.12","gitBranch":"feature/smart-window-management","agentId":"a7b674f","slug":"reactive-snacking-metcalfe","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01A1eHZEZMEcMQbpavt1D9CZ","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# MATRIX WINDOW MONITOR\n     2→# Background process that monitors Matrix window positions and snaps them back when dragged\n     3→# Runs independently of Redpill - starts with bluepill, runs until all Matrix windows close\n     4→\n     5→$matrixDir = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\Documents\\Matrix\"\n     6→\n     7→# Import layout engine\n     8→. \"$PSScriptRoot\\WindowLayoutEngine.ps1\"\n     9→\n    10→# Add Window API\n    11→Add-Type -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue -TypeDefinition @\"\n    12→using System;\n    13→using System.Collections.Generic;\n    14→using System.Runtime.InteropServices;\n    15→using System.Text;\n    16→using System.Diagnostics;\n    17→\n    18→public class MonitorAPI {\n    19→    public delegate bool EnumWindowsProc(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr lParam);\n    20→\n    21→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    22→    public static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumWindowsProc lpEnumFunc, IntPtr lParam);\n    23→\n    24→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", CharSet = CharSet.Auto)]\n    25→    public static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr hWnd, StringBuilder lpString, int nMaxCount);\n    26→\n    27→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    28→    public static extern bool IsWindowVisible(IntPtr hWnd);\n    29→\n    30→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    31→    public static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr hWnd, out uint lpdwProcessId);\n    32→\n    33→    private static List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>> foundWindows;\n    34→\n    35→    public static List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>> FindMatrixWindows() {\n    36→        foundWindows = new List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>>();\n    37→        EnumWindows((hWnd, lParam) => {\n    38→            if (IsWindowVisible(hWnd)) {\n    39→                uint processId;\n    40→                GetWindowThreadProcessId(hWnd, out processId);\n    41→                try {\n    42→                    var process = Process.GetProcessById((int)processId);\n    43→                    if (process.ProcessName.Equals(\"WindowsTerminal\", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) {\n    44→                        var sb = new StringBuilder(256);\n    45→                        GetWindowText(hWnd, sb, 256);\n    46→                        var title = sb.ToString();\n    47→                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(title) && title.Contains(\"Matrix-\") && !title.Contains(\"Redpill\")) {\n    48→                            foundWindows.Add(new KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>(hWnd, title));\n    49→                        }\n    50→                    }\n    51→                } catch { }\n    52→            }\n    53→            return true;\n    54→        }, IntPtr.Zero);\n    55→        return foundWindows;\n    56→    }\n    57→}\n    58→\"@\n    59→\n    60→function Get-MatrixWindowHandles {\n    61→    $windows = [MonitorAPI]::FindMatrixWindows()\n    62→    $result = @()\n    63→    foreach ($w in $windows) {\n    64→        if ($w.Value -match \"Matrix-(\\d+)\") {\n    65→            $result += @{\n    66→                Handle = $w.Key\n    67→                Slot = [int]$Matches[1]\n    68→            }\n    69→        }\n    70→    }\n    71→    return $result\n    72→}\n    73→\n    74→function Position-AllMatrixWindows {\n    75→    $windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowHandles\n    76→    if ($windowInfo.Count -eq 0) { return }\n    77→\n    78→    $windowHandles = @{}\n    79→    foreach ($win in $windowInfo) {\n    80→        $windowHandles[\"Matrix-$($win.Slot)\"] = @{ Handle = $win.Handle }\n    81→    }\n    82→\n    83→    try {\n    84→        $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n    85→        $mode = if ($config.Mode) { $config.Mode } else { 'Pillars' }\n    86→        Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowHandles $windowHandles -Mode $mode | Out-Null\n    87→    }\n    88→    catch { }\n    89→}\n    90→\n    91→# Settings\n    92→$pollIntervalMs = 200   # Check every 200ms\n    93→$dragSettleMs = 400     # Wait after drag detected before repositioning\n    94→$noWindowsTimeout = 5000  # Exit after 5 seconds with no Matrix windows\n    95→\n    96→Write-Host \"Matrix Window Monitor started\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n    97→Write-Host \"Monitoring for window drags... (Ctrl+C to stop)\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    98→\n    99→$noWindowsTime = 0\n   100→$initialized = $false\n   101→\n   102→while ($true) {\n   103→    $windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowHandles\n   104→    $handles = @($windowInfo | ForEach-Object { $_.Handle })\n   105→\n   106→    # Exit if no Matrix windows for too long\n   107→    if ($handles.Count -eq 0) {\n   108→        $noWindowsTime += $pollIntervalMs\n   109→        if ($noWindowsTime -ge $noWindowsTimeout) {\n   110→            Write-Host \"No Matrix windows detected for $($noWindowsTimeout/1000)s. Exiting.\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n   111→            exit 0\n   112→        }\n   113→        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $pollIntervalMs\n   114→        continue\n   115→    }\n   116→    $noWindowsTime = 0\n   117→\n   118→    # Initialize tracking if needed\n   119→    if (-not $initialized -or $handles.Count -ne $script:LastKnownPositions.Count) {\n   120→        Initialize-PositionTracking -WindowHandles $handles\n   121→        $initialized = $true\n   122→    }\n   123→\n   124→    # Check for drag\n   125→    if (Test-WindowDragDetected -WindowHandles $handles) {\n   126→        Write-Host \"Drag detected - repositioning...\" -ForegroundColor Green\n   127→        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $dragSettleMs\n   128→        Position-AllMatrixWindows\n   129→        Update-PositionTracking -WindowHandles $handles\n   130→    }\n   131→\n   132→    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $pollIntervalMs\n   133→}\n   134→\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code MATRIX WINDOW MONITOR\n# Background process that monitors Matrix window positions and snaps them back when dragged\n# Runs independently of Redpill - starts with bluepill, runs until all Matrix windows close\n\n$matrixDir = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\Documents\\Matrix\"\n\n# Import layout engine\n. \"$PSScriptRoot\\WindowLayoutEngine.ps1\"\n\n# Add Window API\nAdd-Type -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue -TypeDefinition @\"\nusing System;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing System.Runtime.InteropServices;\nusing System.Text;\nusing System.Diagnostics;\n\npublic class MonitorAPI {\n    public delegate bool EnumWindowsProc(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr lParam);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    public static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumWindowsProc lpEnumFunc, IntPtr lParam);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", CharSet = CharSet.Auto)]\n    public static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr hWnd, StringBuilder lpString, int nMaxCount);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    public static extern bool IsWindowVisible(IntPtr hWnd);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    public static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr hWnd, out uint lpdwProcessId);\n\n    private static List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>> foundWindows;\n\n    public static List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>> FindMatrixWindows() {\n        foundWindows = new List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>>();\n        EnumWindows((hWnd, lParam) => {\n            if (IsWindowVisible(hWnd)) {\n                uint processId;\n                GetWindowThreadProcessId(hWnd, out processId);\n                try {\n                    var process = Process.GetProcessById((int)processId);\n                    if (process.ProcessName.Equals(\"WindowsTerminal\", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) {\n                        var sb = new StringBuilder(256);\n                        GetWindowText(hWnd, sb, 256);\n                        var title = sb.ToString();\n                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(title) && title.Contains(\"Matrix-\") && !title.Contains(\"Redpill\")) {\n                            foundWindows.Add(new KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>(hWnd, title));\n                        }\n                    }\n                } catch { }\n            }\n            return true;\n        }, IntPtr.Zero);\n        return foundWindows;\n    }\n}\n\"@\n\nfunction Get-MatrixWindowHandles {\n    $windows = [MonitorAPI]::FindMatrixWindows()\n    $result = @()\n    foreach ($w in $windows) {\n        if ($w.Value -match \"Matrix-(\\d+)\") {\n            $result += @{\n                Handle = $w.Key\n                Slot = [int]$Matches[1]\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return $result\n}\n\nfunction Position-AllMatrixWindows {\n    $windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowHandles\n    if ($windowInfo.Count -eq 0) { return }\n\n    $windowHandles = @{}\n    foreach ($win in $windowInfo) {\n        $windowHandles[\"Matrix-$($win.Slot)\"] = @{ Handle = $win.Handle }\n    }\n\n    try {\n        $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n        $mode = if ($config.Mode) { $config.Mode } else { 'Pillars' }\n        Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowHandles $windowHandles -Mode $mode | Out-Null\n    }\n    catch { }\n}\n\n# Settings\n$pollIntervalMs = 200   # Check every 200ms\n$dragSettleMs = 400     # Wait after drag detected before repositioning\n$noWindowsTimeout = 5000  # Exit after 5 seconds with no Matrix windows\n\nWrite-Host \"Matrix Window Monitor started\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\nWrite-Host \"Monitoring for window drags... (Ctrl+C to stop)\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n\n$noWindowsTime = 0\n$initialized = $false\n\nwhile ($true) {\n    $windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowHandles\n    $handles = @($windowInfo | ForEach-Object { $_.Handle })\n\n    # Exit if no Matrix windows for too long\n    if ($handles.Count -eq 0) {\n        $noWindowsTime += $pollIntervalMs\n        if ($noWindowsTime -ge $noWindowsTimeout) {\n            Write-Host \"No Matrix windows detected for $($noWindowsTimeout/1000)s. Exiting.\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n            exit 0\n        }\n        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $pollIntervalMs\n        continue\n    }\n    $noWindowsTime = 0\n\n    # Initialize tracking if needed\n    if (-not $initialized -or $handles.Count -ne $script:LastKnownPositions.Count) {\n        Initialize-PositionTracking -WindowHandles $handles\n        $initialized = $true\n    }\n\n    # Check for drag\n    if (Test-WindowDragDetected -WindowHandles $handles) {\n        Write-Host \"Drag detected - repositioning...\" -ForegroundColor Green\n        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $dragSettleMs\n        Position-AllMatrixWindows\n        Update-PositionTracking -WindowHandles $handles\n    }\n\n    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $pollIntervalMs\n}\n","numLines":134,"startLine":1,"totalLines":134}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ffb046d0-40e7-465c-bdc3-5f828254d7b0"}

{"parentUuid":"20ab128e-9888-46ac-8a82-bef54a9eb687","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX","sessionId":"7950de6a-373b-44cb-ac45-a9b755daa3bc","version":"2.1.12","gitBranch":"feature/smart-window-management","agentId":"a7b674f","slug":"reactive-snacking-metcalfe","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_01LbRSp22byKrxf2qGGu1mv7","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Perfect. Now I have comprehensive understanding of the layout distribution and positioning algorithms. Let me compile my findings into a detailed report.\n\n## Matrix Terminal - Layout Distribution and Positioning Analysis\n\nBased on my exploration of the WindowLayoutEngine.ps1 and matrix_control.ps1 files, here's a detailed report on the current distribution logic, monitor prioritization, layout calculations, and algorithm gaps:\n\n---\n\n### 1. WINDOW DISTRIBUTION ALGORITHMS\n\n#### **Get-WindowDistributionWithPrimary (Lines 396-478)** - PRIMARY DISTRIBUTION FUNCTION\nThis is the main distribution function used by layout strategies. It has three modes:\n\n**Auto Mode (WindowsOnPrimary = null):**\n- Balanced distribution: `floor(WindowCount / ScreenCount)` + round-robin remainder\n- Each screen gets `base_count`, first screens get +1 for remainder\n- Example: 5 windows on 2 screens → [3, 2]\n- All capped at MaxPerScreen (default: 4 for Pillars, 4 for Quads)\n\n**User-Specified Mode (WindowsOnPrimary = integer):**\n- Primary screen (index 0) gets exactly N windows\n- Remaining windows distributed to secondary screens via ceiling division\n- Example: 5 windows, WindowsOnPrimary=3 on 2 screens → [3, 2]\n- If WindowsOnPrimary=0 → all on secondary screens\n\n**Single Screen:**\n- All windows go to that screen (no capping enforced, allows overflow)\n- Handled separately to allow multi-row layouts\n\n#### **Get-WindowDistribution (Lines 292-354)** - LEGACY FUNCTION\n- Simpler balanced distribution without primary screen preference\n- Used only by some legacy code paths\n- Base algorithm: `floor(WindowCount / ScreenCount)` + distribute remainder round-robin\n\n---\n\n### 2. SCREEN TOPOLOGY DETECTION & MONITOR PRIORITIZATION\n\n#### **Get-ScreenTopology (Lines 203-252)**\n```\nPriority: PRIMARY FIRST, then LEFT-TO-RIGHT\n```\n\n**Detection Process:**\n1. Uses `System.Windows.Forms.Screen.AllScreens` API\n2. Gets WorkingArea (excludes taskbar, docked windows)\n3. Returns:\n   - Index, Left, Top, Width, Height, IsPrimary for each screen\n\n**Sorting Strategy (CRITICAL):**\n```powershell\nSort by:\n  1. IsPrimary DESC (true first = -[int]$_.IsPrimary)\n  2. Left ASC (left-to-right)\n```\n\n**Result:** Windows' actual primary monitor is ALWAYS at index 0, then secondary monitors sorted left-to-right.\n\n**Example:** On a 3-monitor setup with secondary-primary-secondary layout:\n- Physical order: Monitor2 (1920,0) | Monitor1-PRIMARY (0,0) | Monitor3 (3840,0)\n- Detected order: Index 0=PRIMARY (0,0), Index 1=Monitor2 (1920,0), Index 2=Monitor3 (3840,0)\n\n---\n\n### 3. LAYOUT CALCULATION ALGORITHMS\n\n#### **Get-PillarsLayout (Lines 514-606)** - VERTICAL COLUMNS\n\n**Layout Strategy:**\n- Windows arranged as vertical columns (pillars) side-by-side\n- Multi-row support: MaxPillarsPerScreen columns with overflow to additional rows\n\n**Calculation Steps:**\n```\n1. Distribute windows using Get-WindowDistributionWithPrimary\n2. For each screen:\n   - Columns = min(windowsOnScreen, MaxPillarsPerScreen)\n   - Rows = ceil(windowsOnScreen / Columns)\n3. Calculate cell dimensions (fills screen):\n   - Total gaps: (N+1) gaps for N columns/rows (edge gaps + internal gaps)\n   - cellWidth = (screenWidth - (columns+1)*gap) / columns\n   - cellHeight = (screenHeight - (rows+1)*gap) / rows\n4. Place windows in grid using column-major order:\n   - Position: x = screenLeft + gap + (col * (cellWidth + gap))\n   - Position: y = screenTop + gap + (row * (cellHeight + gap))\n```\n\n**Example (1920x1080, 4 windows, 4 columns, 60px gap):**\n```\nGaps: 5×60 = 300 horizontal, 5×60 = 300 vertical\nCell width: (1920 - 300) / 4 = 405px\nCell height: (1080 - 300) / 1 = 780px\nWindow 0: (60, 60) 405×780\nWindow 1: (525, 60) 405×780\nWindow 2: (990, 60) 405×780\nWindow 3: (1455, 60) 405×780\n```\n\n#### **Get-QuadsLayout (Lines 665-802)** - 2×2 GRID WITH PLUS-GAP\n\n**Layout Strategy:**\n- Maximum 4 windows per screen in 2×2 grid\n- Distinctive plus-shaped (+) gap in center\n- Fallback to extended grid for >4 windows per screen\n\n**Calculation for Standard Quad (≤4 windows):**\n```\nPlus-gap formula: 3 gaps per dimension (edge + center + edge)\n- halfWidth = (screenWidth - 3*gap) / 2\n- halfHeight = (screenHeight - 3*gap) / 2\n\nQuad positions (TL, TR, BL, BR):\n- TL: (screenLeft + gap, screenTop + gap)\n- TR: (screenLeft + 2*gap + halfWidth, screenTop + gap)\n- BL: (screenLeft + gap, screenTop + 2*gap + halfHeight)\n- BR: (screenLeft + 2*gap + halfWidth, screenTop + 2*gap + halfHeight)\n```\n\n**Example (1920x1080, 4 windows, 60px gap):**\n```\nhalfWidth = (1920 - 180) / 2 = 870px\nhalfHeight = (1080 - 180) / 2 = 450px\nTL: (60, 60) 870×450\nTR: (990, 60) 870×450\nBL: (60, 570) 870×450\nBR: (990, 570) 870×450\n```\n\n**Overflow Handling (>4 windows):**\n- Falls back to extended grid layout\n- Calculates grid dimensions: `cols = ceil(sqrt(windowCount * (width/height)))`\n- Maintains roughly square cells\n- Uses same gap logic as Pillars\n\n---\n\n### 4. ORCHESTRATION: Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout (Lines 997-1138)\n\n**Main Entry Point:**\n```\nInput: WindowHandles hashtable {name → {Handle, ProcessId}}\nOutput: Array of {Name, Handle, X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex}\n```\n\n**Process:**\n1. **Filter Valid Windows:**\n   - Check if handle exists and is visible\n   - Restore minimized windows\n   - Build array of sortedWindows by name (Matrix-1, Matrix-2, etc.)\n\n2. **Determine Mode:**\n   - Uses config.Mode if set (Pillars/Quads)\n   - Falls back to Auto: Pillars (≤4 windows) or Quads (>4 windows)\n\n3. **Get Screen Topology:**\n   - Calls Get-ScreenTopology\n   - Handles fallback to primary screen only if detection fails\n\n4. **Calculate Layout:**\n   - Calls Get-PillarsLayout or Get-QuadsLayout\n   - Passes config.WindowsOnPrimary for distribution control\n\n5. **Apply Positioning (unless DryRun):**\n   - Uses SetWindowPos with SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_SHOWWINDOW flags\n   - Updates position tracking for drag detection\n\n---\n\n### 5. WINDOWS-ON-PRIMARY SETTING (Multi-Monitor Control)\n\n**Configuration:**\n- Stored in matrix_state.json → `layout.windowsOnPrimary`\n- Default: `null` (Auto mode)\n- Valid values: null/-1 (Auto), 0 (all secondary), 1..N (explicit count)\n\n**UI Controls (matrix_control.ps1 Lines 997-1048):**\n- `[<]` (comma): Decrease windows on primary\n- `[>]` (period): Increase windows on primary\n- `[)]` (shift+0): Reset to Auto\n- Display shows \"Auto\" when null, actual count otherwise\n\n**Behavior:**\n```\nAuto (null): Balanced across all screens\n  5 windows, 2 screens → [3, 2]\n\nExplicit (3): Primary gets 3, rest overflow\n  5 windows, 2 screens, Primary=3 → [3, 2]\n\nZero (0): All secondary\n  5 windows, 2 screens, Primary=0 → [0, 5]\n```\n\n---\n\n### 6. CURRENT ALGORITHM GAPS & LIMITATIONS\n\n#### **Gap 1: No Monitor Capacity Awareness**\n- Algorithm divides windows equally but doesn't consider monitor resolution differences\n- A 1920×1080 and 3440×1440 ultra-wide get same window count\n- No scaling of window sizes by monitor capabilities\n\n#### **Gap 2: Static MaxPerScreen Cap**\n- Hardcoded to 4 windows per screen (Pillars and Quads)\n- No consideration for monitor resolution or user preferences\n- Can't dynamically increase to 6 or 9 windows based on screen real estate\n\n#### **Gap 3: No Aspect Ratio Optimization**\n- Cell dimensions calculated naively: `(width - gaps) / columns`\n- Doesn't respect or maintain target aspect ratios\n- Ultra-wide monitors get stretched small windows instead of utilizing width\n\n#### **Gap 4: No Monitor Rotation Detection**\n- Algorithm assumes landscape orientation\n- Portrait monitors would have incorrect height/width ratios\n- Gap calculations don't account for rotated displays\n\n#### **Gap 5: Rigid Distribution Strategy**\n- Only two modes: Pillars (all vertical) and Quads (all 2×2)\n- No hybrid layouts (e.g., 2 on primary in quads, 3 on secondary in pillars)\n- No layout suggestions based on window count and screen count\n\n#### **Gap 6: Single WindowsOnPrimary for All Layouts**\n- Same primary allocation applies to both Pillars and Quads\n- No per-layout customization (e.g., primary=2 for Quads, primary=4 for Pillars)\n\n#### **Gap 7: No Performance/Density Tiers**\n- No \"compact,\" \"balanced,\" or \"spacious\" modes\n- Gap size is global; can't per-screen customize\n- No consideration for reading distance or window content type\n\n#### **Gap 8: No Persistence of Per-Monitor Layouts**\n- WindowsOnPrimary is global setting\n- If user arranges windows manually, layout restores to config, not last user positions\n- Save-WindowPositions exists but not integrated into layout logic\n\n#### **Gap 9: No Drag-Snapping Back to Original Layout**\n- matrix_monitor.ps1 repositions to layout when drag detected\n- User can't escape layout (good for consistency, bad for flexibility)\n- No \"unlock\" mode for temporary manual arrangement\n\n#### **Gap 10: No Layout Validation or Conflict Resolution**\n- If window count changes mid-layout, algorithm handles it but doesn't validate result\n- No check for overlapping windows or off-screen positioning\n- No fallback if monitor detection fails during layout application\n\n---\n\n### 7. INTEGRATION POINTS\n\n**Files that use layout engine:**\n\n1. **matrix_control.ps1** (Lines 338, 784-802, 916-924, 997-1048)\n   - Shift+L toggles Pillars ↔ Quads\n   - < and > adjust WindowsOnPrimary\n   - Position-MatrixWindows applies layout after window launch\n\n2. **matrix_setup.ps1** (setup wizard)\n   - Calls WindowLayoutEngine after launching windows\n   - Applies layout to newly created Matrix windows\n\n3. **bluepill.ps1** (entry point)\n   - Integrates WindowLayoutEngine for automatic positioning\n   - Uses layout configuration from matrix_state.json\n\n4. **matrix_monitor.ps1** (Lines 74-89)\n   - Watches for window drag events\n   - Reapplies layout when drag detected (snaps back to grid)\n\n---\n\n### 8. DATA FLOW SUMMARY\n\n```\nUser Input (Shift+L, <, >)\n    ↓\nmatrix_control.ps1 → Set-MatrixLayoutConfig\n    ↓\nmatrix_state.json {layout: {mode, windowsOnPrimary, ...}}\n    ↓\nPosition-MatrixWindows → Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout\n    ↓\nGet-ScreenTopology → Get-PillarsLayout / Get-QuadsLayout\n    ↓\nGet-WindowDistributionWithPrimary\n    ↓\nSetWindowPos via Windows API\n    ↓\nWindows Terminal windows repositioned\n    ↓\nmatrix_monitor.ps1 updates position tracking\n```\n\n---\n\n### 9. KEY FINDINGS FOR SMART ACCOMMODATION\n\n**To implement \"smart accommodation\" that adapts to monitor capabilities:**\n\n1. **Extend Get-ScreenTopology** to return DPI, resolution categories (small/large/ultra-wide)\n2. **Create Monitor-Aware Distribution** that considers resolution ratios, not just count\n3. **Implement Density Modes** (compact/balanced/spacious) that adjust gap sizes and MaxPerScreen\n4. **Add Per-Monitor Customization** so large monitors can hold 6+ windows while small hold 2\n5. **Support Hybrid Layouts** where primary and secondary screens use different strategies\n6. **Persist User Overrides** separately from layout config (manual position snapshots)\n7. **Add Layout Suggestions** algorithm that recommends optimal distribution for given window count\n8. **Support Monitor Rotation** detection and layout validation\n\nCurrent algorithm is deterministic, predictable, and consistent—but treats all monitors equally regardless of actual capacity."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":7,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3554,"cache_read_input_tokens":58878,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3554,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CXHJXvLyQa74PiTWuXhCk","type":"assistant","uuid":"f2ce4c42-95c1-4e60-863b-c3bd6b4d4764","timestamp":"2026-01-19T19:39:43.275Z"}
